*Marius Mönch,Nicole Marheineke,Andreas Bartel,Kevin Schäfers,Michael Günther*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于算子分裂法求解耦合端口哈密顿系统的能量一致性问题，提出以能量一致性作为评估标准，并分析了不同分解策略对能量行为和计算效率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于端口哈密顿系统的分解方式不唯一，不同分解可能显著影响计算效率和能量行为的保持，因此需要系统性地研究分裂方法与能量一致性的关系。

Method: 引入能量一致性准则，推导出分裂方法继承原系统能量行为的充分条件，并分析多种分解策略，包括保持结构、利用低维子系统动态或时间尺度分离的方法，结合Strang分裂及其多时间步长扩展进行数值实验。

Result: 保持端口哈密顿结构的子流是实现能量一致性分裂的关键；利用时间尺度分离的分解在多速率特征明显的系统中可大幅提升计算效率，而破坏结构的分解可能导致不良的能量行为。

Conclusion: 在保证能量一致性和计算效率之间需权衡，保持子系统结构的分裂更利于能量守恒，而基于时间尺度的分解更适合多速率系统以提升效率。

Abstract: Operator splitting provides an attractive approach for the numerical integration of (coupled) port-Hamiltonian systems, as it allows the underlying system structure to be exploited at the level of the individual subproblems. However, the choice of the decomposition is not unique and may strongly affect both the computational efficiency and the preservation of the energy behavior of the original system. In this work, we investigate this interplay systematically and introduce energy consistency as a criterion for assessing splitting methods for port-Hamiltonian ordinary differential equations. We derive sufficient conditions under which a splitting based on a given decomposition inherits the energy behavior of the continuous system and use these conditions to analyze several decomposition strategies for coupled port-Hamiltonian systems. In particular, we compare decompositions that preserve the structure with approaches that exploit lower-dimensional subsystem dynamics or separated time scales. The analysis is complemented by numerical experiments using Strang splitting and its multiple-time-stepping extension. A scalable electro-thermal benchmark with fast electrical and slow thermal dynamics is employed to assess accuracy, energy behavior, and computational efficiency. The results demonstrate that preserving the port-Hamiltonian structure of the subflows is essential for energy-consistent splitting, whereas decompositions that exploit subsystem structure or time-scale separation can provide substantial computational advantages. In particular, the time-scale decomposition yields significant efficiency gains for systems with pronounced multirate characteristics, while structure-destroying decompositions may lead to undesirable energy behavior.

</details>

*Franziska Eickmann,Tabea Tscherpel*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一框架，用于为Stokes方程的协调混合有限元对构造局部Fortin算子，满足散度保持、局部稳定性、逼近性和迹保持等性质，并讨论了其在非牛顿流体、非齐次边界条件和移动域等问题中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提供一种通用且系统的方法来构造满足关键数学性质的局部Fortin算子，从而确保混合有限元方法在Stokes方程中的稳定性和收敛性。

Method: 通过设计满足散度保持、局部稳定性、逼近性和迹保持性质的局部Fortin算子，并在多种经典有限元对（如P_d-P_0、Bernardi-Raugel、Crouzeix-Raviart等）上实现，必要时对速度空间进行 enrichment。

Result: 成功构建了适用于多种协调混合有限元对的局部Fortin算子，证明了其在不同元素上的可行性，并展示了该框架的广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 该统一框架不仅保证了inf-sup稳定性，还为处理非牛顿流体、非齐次边界条件及移动域问题提供了理论基础和工具支持。

Abstract: We present a unifying framework to construct local Fortin operators for conforming mixed finite element pairs for the Stokes equations. The operators are constructed to satisfy the divergence-preservation property, local stability and approximation properties, and certain trace-preservation properties. For the latter, some of the finite element pairs require an enrichment of the velocity space. We present the construction for the $P_d-P_0$ element, the Bernardi-Raugel element, the conforming Crouzeix-Raviart element, a modified MINI element and generalised Taylor-Hood elements. Furthermore, we discuss implications of the existence of such a Fortin operator beyond inf-sup stability. These include applications to non-Newtonian fluid flow, problems with inhomogeneous Dirichlet boundary conditions, as well as uniform inf-sup stability relevant for domain approximation and moving domains.

</details>


### [92] [Frequency-explicit convergence analysis of a multiscale finite element method for highly heterogeneous scattering problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04930)
*T. Chaumont-Frelet,Z. Kassali*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种高阶多尺度有限元方法，用于分析高频下高度异质可穿透障碍物的时谐散射问题，并给出了显式依赖于波数和材料特征尺寸的误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高频情况下，传统方法难以有效处理包含多种材料的高度异质散射体，需要发展新的数值方法以提高计算效率和精度。

Method: 提出一种高阶多尺度有限元方法，并进行显式关于波数k和材料特征尺寸ε的误差分析。

Result: 误差估计表明，使用高阶方法可以降低大频率下的计算成本，数值实验验证了这一结论。

Conclusion: 该高阶多尺度有限元方法在处理高频、多材料散射问题时具有良好的理论支持和实际效果，能显著提升计算效率。

Abstract: We analyze the numerical approximation of time-harmonic scattering by highly heterogeneous penetrable obstacles. These problems are especially challenging in the high-frequency regime, where the size of the scatterer $L$ is much larger than the wavelength, i.e., the wavenumber $k$ is such that $kL \gg 1$. Here, we further consider the situation where the scatterer contains different materials, with a characteristic size $\varepsilon$ such that $k\varepsilon \ll 1$. We propose a high-order multiscale finite element method, and provide an error analysis that is explicit in both $k$ and $\varepsilon$. Crucially, our error estimates suggest that using a high-order method should reduce the computational cost for large frequencies, which is corroborated by numerical examples.

</details>


### [93] [A simple shallow neural network for emulating the solution to singularly perturbed problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05180)
*Christos Xenophontos,Aayushman Raina*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种基于浅层神经网络的数值方法，用于求解具有两个小参数的奇异摄动二阶边值问题，通过引入渐近展开和指数函数增强逼近空间，实现对参数鲁棒的高精度模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对含有多个小参数的奇异摄动问题，传统神经网络难以捕捉边界层行为，因此需要结合渐近分析构造更具适应性的逼近结构。

Method: 利用解的渐近展开将其分解为光滑部分和边界层部分，构建带有tanh激活函数的浅层神经网络，并在逼近空间中加入适当的指数函数以增强表达能力，采用残差最小化或能量最小化等训练目标。

Result: 在一维和二维（包括非张量积光滑区域）数值算例中验证了该方法的有效性，表现出对不同摄动参数范围的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过融合渐近分析与神经网络逼近，所提方法能有效处理多参数奇异摄动问题，在无需自适应网格的情况下实现稳定且高精度的解模拟。

Abstract: We consider (feed-forward) Neural Networks (NNs) for the emulation of the solution to singularly perturbed second order boundary value problems, with two small parameters. We describe a shallow NN which exploits available asymptotic expansions for the solution. These additive decompositions into smooth and layer components, allow for derivative estimates which are explicit in the order of differentiation as well as the singular perturbation parameter(s) \cite{melenk, Irene, SX}. Utilizing such decompositions, we propose a simple NN for emulating the solution to such problems using the $\tanh$ activation function together with different training objectives, such as residual or energy minimization. The key idea is to augment the approximation space with suitable exponential functions, similar to enriched spaces in finite element methods, e.g.~\cite{Kellogg}. Numerical examples in one and two dimensions, including a smooth non-tensor-product domain, illustrate the resulting parameter-robust behavior over the tested perturbation ranges.

</details>


### [94] [A Generalized Scalar Auxiliary Variable Method for Structure-Preserving and Efficient Integration of Nonlinear port-Hamiltonian DAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05246)
*Aashutosh Sharma,Andreas Bartel,Manuel Schaller*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于非线性指数一端口哈密顿微分代数方程的能量最优广义标量辅助变量（EOP-GSAV）框架，该方法结合BDF格式实现了每步仅需一次线性求解且保持离散无源性和能量追踪，数值实验表明其在精度匹配下具有显著效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非线性指数一pH-DAE系统，传统方法在保持结构特性和计算效率方面存在挑战，因此需要发展既能保持系统端口哈密顿结构又具备高计算效率的数值方法。

Method: 利用端口哈密顿结构将非线性项分解为恒定隐式核心与可分离的非线性努力、互连和耗散部分，构建EOP-GSAV框架，并设计基于BDF-1和BDF-2的数值格式，仅需每次时间步一次带可重用因子化的线性求解。

Result: 所提方法在多种数值实验中表现出稳健性和竞争力，在相同精度下相比使用不同雅可比处理策略的隐式中点法有显著效率提升；与SUNDIALS IDA相比，在同等阶数下工作精度表现相当，尽管实现未专门优化。

Conclusion: EOP-GSAV框架为非线性pH-DAEs提供了一类高效、结构保持的数值解法，具有良好的实际应用前景，尤其在中等至高精度需求场景下表现突出。

Abstract: We develop an energy-optimal generalized scalar auxiliary variable (EOP-GSAV) framework for nonlinear index-one port-Hamiltonian differential-algebraic equations (pH-DAEs). Exploiting the port-Hamiltonian structure, we separate the nonlinear effort, interconnection, and dissipation terms from a constant implicit core. The resulting BDF-1 and BDF-2 schemes require one linear solve per time step with a reusable factorization, while retaining discrete passivity and accurate tracking of the Hamiltonian. The schemes are compared with the implicit midpoint method equipped with full, modified, and frozen-Jacobian Newton iterations. Numerical experiments ranging from a strongly state-dependent nonlinear stress test to large-scale benchmarks demonstrate robust and competitive performance, with substantial efficiency gains in matched-accuracy regimes. A comparison with SUNDIALS IDA shows comparable work-precision behavior at equal order despite a non-specialized Python/SciPy implementation, while unrestricted variable-order adaptive IDA is faster in the high-accuracy regime.

</details>


### [95] [Shallow neural network approximation in mixed Sobolev spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05263)
*Yuwen Li,Guozhi Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了浅层神经网络在混合Sobolev空间中对$L_2$逼近的最优性，提出了与激活函数无关的Fourier-block准则，并针对不同激活函数（如ReLU、B样条、ELU等）给出了逼近阶的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解浅层神经网络在混合光滑性函数空间中的逼近能力，特别是不同激活函数对逼近速率的影响，本文旨在建立一个统一的理论框架。

Method: 引入了一个与激活函数无关的Fourier-block原理，并提出一种结构化的单变量逼近条件来验证具体激活函数的Fourier-block性质；通过分析不同激活函数的逼近性能得出代数逼近阶。

Result: 建立了激活函数无关的Fourier-block准则，证明了对于具有近似阶ρ的激活函数，全局逼近速率为min{α,ρ}（最多差对数因子）；对ReLU^k、B样条和soft-ReLU^k得到最优代数逼近阶min{α,k+1}，对ELU和余弦激活函数达到完整的混合光滑性阶α。

Conclusion: 该框架统一解释了多种激活函数在混合Sobolev空间中的$L_2$逼近性能，揭示了光滑性和激活函数近似能力之间的权衡关系，并在对数因子意义下确立了若干常见激活函数的最优逼近阶。

Abstract: We investigate the best $L_2$ approximation of mixed Sobolev spaces by shallow neural networks with $n$ neurons and general activation functions. We first establish an activation-independent Fourier-block principle: if an activation has univariate approximation order $ρ$ in the sense of the Fourier-block property, then the global approximation rate has algebraic order $\min\{α,ρ\}$ for target functions of mixed smoothness $α$, up to explicit logarithmic factors. To verify this property for concrete activations, we introduce a structured univariate approximation condition that implies the Fourier-block property with explicit parameters. For $\mathrm{ReLU}^k$, a matching algebraic lower bound identifies $\min\{α,k+1\}$ as the optimal algebraic approximation exponent in any dimension, up to logarithmic factors in the upper bound. The framework also yields the exponent $\min\{α,k+1\}$ for cardinal B-splines and soft-$\mathrm{ReLU}^k$, and the full mixed-smoothness exponent $α$ for ELU and cosine activations, again up to logarithmic~factors.

</details>

### [96] [Niels Bohr as a Physicist of Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04297)
*Mauro Dorato,Jan Faye*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 玻尔采用了一种原理性方法来调和量子力学中的两个原则：区分原则与非分离原则，强调测量过程的物理不可逆性，并拒绝为测量提供建构性解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在阐明玻尔如何通过原理性方法解决量子系统与经典仪器之间认识论区分与本体论不可分之间的张力。

Method: 分析玻尔关于测量相互作用是一种物理且不可逆过程的观点，揭示其对量子与经典领域无本质区别的立场。

Result: 表明玻尔拒绝诸如自发动力学还原模型等对测量过程的建构性说明，因其坚持认识论上的区分必要性与本体论上的非分离性之间的平衡。

Conclusion: 玻尔的量子力学诠释是一种原则性理论，其核心在于协调认识论需求与本体论现实之间的矛盾，而非构建具体的物理机制。

Abstract: Our main claim is that Bohr adopted a principle-theoretic approach to quantum mechanics in order to reconcile two principles: the distinction principle, which asserts the necessity of a clear epistemic distinction between a classical apparatus and a quantum system, and the non-separability principle, which asserts that the two systems are ontologically non-separable in any measurement process. The latter principle is a consequence of Bohr's argument that, during a measurement interaction, the quantum system and the measurement apparatus form a 'new kind of individuality'. To support our argument, we first demonstrate the overlooked fact that, according to Bohr, the measurement interaction is a physical, irreversible process and not merely an epistemic acquisition of new information. In this respect, quantum mechanics must be regarded not only as a universal theory, but also as revealing no non-arbitrary difference between a quantum realm and a classical realm; the distinction is purely pragmatic. The tension between the epistemic necessity to rely on a neatly distinguishable classical realm and the ontic non-separability of the quantum system and the classical apparatus inclined Bohr to refuse to provide a constructive account of the measurement process, such as that later proposed, for instance, by spontaneous dynamical reduction models.

</details>


### [97] [From the Light Quantum to the Photon: The Evolution of a Physical Concept](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04985)
*Aaron Collavini,Valentina Bologna,Francesco Longo,Stefano Ansoldi,Fulvio Parmigiani*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 本文追溯了光量子从普朗克黑体理论到电磁场量子化的物理与概念演变，指出爱因斯坦1916-1917年的辐射量子理论是关键转折点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阐明光量子概念如何从争议性假设演变为电磁场量子化的基础。

Method: 通过历史与理论分析，考察从黑体辐射到量子电动力学发展过程中的关键理论突破。

Result: 揭示自发辐射现象暴露了经典辐射描述的不足，推动了电磁场的量子化。

Conclusion: 光量子的物理必要性在理论完善前已被隐含证实，其整合标志着量子理论发展的关键阶段。

Abstract: This work examines the physical and conceptual evolution of the light quantum from Planck's blackbody theory to the theoretical and experimental developments that led to the quantization of the electromagnetic field. The present study shows that the decisive transition occurred in Einstein's quantum theory of radiation (1916-1917). Absorption, stimulated emission, and spontaneous emission were formulated as elementary probabilistic mechanisms whose statistical balance alone reproduces the blackbody spectrum. In particular, spontaneous emission requires the emission of a light quantum, thereby implicitly proving its physical necessity before its theoretical status was clarified. At the same time, the already existing term photon began to acquire a stable usage following Lewis's 1926 proposal and became increasingly associated with Einstein's light quantum. By the mid-1920s, the central problem had shifted from whether light quanta were physically required to how radiation could be incorporated into the emerging quantum-mechanical formalism. This transition marks a key stage, illustrating how initial debates about the existence of light quanta gave way to their integration into a comprehensive theoretical structure. The resulting asymmetry between the novel quantum description of matter and the still-classical description of radiation, called into question by the phenomenon of spontaneous emission, identifies the physical problem that led to the quantization of the electromagnetic field.

</details>

### [98] [STEMPix: A Phase-Transition-Material-Based Pixel Sensor for Resolving Edge-Movement Direction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04435)
*Md Rahatul Islam Udoy,Sumeet Kumar Gupta,Deep Jariwala,Ahmedullah Aziz*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于CMOS图像传感器阵列内的时空边缘运动方向像素（STEMPix），可生成紧凑的方向感知边缘运动信息，适用于重视局部边界运动的专用传感应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像传感器需传输完整的多比特帧进行外部处理，导致数据量大、功耗高；而许多专用应用更关注局部边缘运动而非完整图像重建，因此需要一种能在传感器内部直接提取方向感知边缘运动信息的高效方案。

Method: 设计了一种名为STEMPix的像素结构，通过将像素级时间变化信息与邻近像素的空间边缘信息结合，生成3位的局部边缘方向码（LEDC）；采用光电二极管层与计算层分离的两层架构，在保证光收集面积的同时集成片上处理电路，并支持全局快门和动态阈值调节。

Result: 基于HSPICE瞬态仿真评估显示，该设计实现1.73 μm的水平间距、2.36 μm的垂直间距，几何填充率为95.47%；在典型边缘运动场景下，每次LEDC操作的平均有源开关能耗为0.465 fJ。

Conclusion: STEMPix能够为感知边缘运动的系统提供紧凑且可扩展的前端表示方法，显著降低数据带宽和功耗，适用于低功耗视觉传感等应用场景。

Abstract: This paper proposes a spatio-temporal edge-movement direction pixel (STEMPix) for generating compact direction-aware edge movement information inside a CMOS-compatible image sensor array. The proposed design targets specialized sensing applications where local boundary movement is more important than full-frame intensity reconstruction. Instead of transferring full multi-bit frames for external processing, STEMPix generates a 3-bit local edge direction code (LEDC) by combining pixel-level temporal change information with neighboring-pixel spatial edge information. We design the architecture using a two-tier organization, where the photodiode layer is separated from the computation layer to preserve light-collection area while accommodating the additional in-array processing circuitry. The proposed circuit is evaluated through HSPICE transient simulations. The estimated implementation achieves a horizontal pitch of 1.73 μm, a vertical pitch of 2.36 μm, and a geometric fill factor of 95.47%. The average active switching energy is 0.465 fJ per LEDC operation across representative edge-movement cases. The proposed STEMPix operation also supports global-shutter capture and dynamic thresholding. These results indicate that STEMPix can provide a compact and scalable front-end representation for edge-movement-aware sensing systems.

</details>


### [99] [Beyond Maintenance Manual Multimodal RAG: Suggesting What Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05116)
*Seongjun Ha,Md Rashedul Islam*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: MRAG-SWAT是一种扩展的多模态检索增强生成框架，可同时返回飞机维护任务所需的专用工具和手动工具信息，减少工具查找时间并降低操作风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞机维修人员在执行任务时需频繁查阅维护手册（MM），但手册中未明确列出手动工具需求，且专用工具信息仅在具体步骤中出现，导致工具准备效率低且易出错。

Method: 提出MRAG-SWAT框架，扩展现有的多模态检索增强生成（MRAG）流程，在检索维修程序和对应图示的同时，结合 Illustrated Parts Catalog (IPC) 数据提取硬件尺寸，推断所需的手动工具，并整合特殊工具信息一并返回。

Result: 该框架已在Lycoming IO-360-N1A发动机上实现，并通过八个测试查询验证，能够准确返回维修步骤及相应工具列表。

Conclusion: MRAG-SWAT有助于减少维修人员往返工具间次数，避免因工具选择不当造成的航空器损坏，提升维修效率与飞行安全性。

Abstract: Aircraft technicians are required to consult the maintenance manual (MM) for nearly every task, and locating the relevant procedure across hundreds of pages remains time-consuming. Multimodal retrieval augmented generation (MRAG) has been proposed to address this, allowing technicians to retrieve procedures, together with the accompanying figures, through natural-language queries. However, retrieval alone does not tell the technicians which tools the task requires. The MM identifies special tools only when the corresponding step is reached, and it does not state hand tool requirements at all; to select hand tools, technicians are required to find the hardware dimension from the illustrated parts catalog (IPC) and infer the right tool from it. We therefore propose MRAG-SWAT, an extension of the MRAG pipeline that returns the required hand tools and special tools alongside the retrieved procedure. The framework was implemented for the Lycoming IO-360-N1A engine and demonstrated on eight test queries. By presenting the correct tools together with the procedure, MRAG-SWAT may help reduce repeated trips to the tool crib, prevent damage to aircraft caused by improper tool selection, and thereby avoid additional maintenance tasks and support continued airworthiness.

</details>


### [100] [Hatebench in the era of safer LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05169)
*Ole Becker,Tobias Jongen,Philip Kolbe,Sonal Khosla,Vaibhav Bajpai*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本论文对Shen等人提出的HateBench研究进行了可重复性验证，评估现有仇恨言论检测器在现代大语言模型（LLM）生成内容下的有效性，并检验其结果是否随时间及技术演进而保持稳定。研究重建了原始数据生成流程，纳入新发布的模型和更新的检测器版本。结果显示，新型LLM已引入防护机制以减少有害内容生成；尽管原始研究结果略有高估，但整体结论仍可复现。此外，新版本的omni-Moderation在对抗恶意仇恨活动方面表现出比text-Moderation更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLMs）广泛用于自动化内容生成，其可能被滥用产生仇恨言论，威胁数字安全。现有仇恨言论检测器主要基于人类撰写的文本训练，其对LLM生成内容的有效性尚不明确。因此，有必要验证先前研究结果的可重复性和持久性，并评估检测系统在当前技术环境下的实际表现。

Method: 本研究独立重构了HateBench论文中的数据集生成流程，使用现代大语言模型重新生成测试数据，并扩展基准以涵盖最新发布的LLM和更新版本的检测器。通过复现实验评估检测器对LLM生成仇恨内容的识别能力，特别关注两种复杂类型的仇恨运动。同时比较了text-Moderation与新版omni-Moderation在面对对抗性攻击时的表现差异。

Result: 研究发现，较新的LLM已内置安全机制，显著降低有害内容的生成可能性。对于复杂的仇恨言论类型，原始研究的结果虽因数据偏见而略有高估，但整体趋势得以复现。新版omni-Moderation相比text-Moderation在抵御对抗性仇恨内容方面展现出轻微但可测的性能提升。

Conclusion: 尽管原始HateBench研究的部分结果存在一定程度的高估，但其核心结论——即现有仇恨言论检测器在应对LLM生成内容时存在局限——仍然成立。本研究证实了这些漏洞具有一定的持久性，为内容审核技术的长期评估提供了实证依据，并强调需持续更新检测方法以应对不断演变的生成模型和攻击策略。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) lower the barrier for au- tomated content generation, the potential for producing hate speech poses a significant challenge for digital safety. This paper presents a reproducibility study of the HateBench paper by Shen et al., investigating whether existing hate speech detectors, typically trained on human-authored data, generalize to LLM-generated hateful content, and evaluating whether their reported weaknesses are stable over time and robust to evolving components. We independently reconstruct the original dataset genera- tion pipeline using modern LLMs and extend the benchmark to include recently released models and updated detector versions. Our independent assessment under current con- ditions finds that for newer LLMs, safeguards have been put into place to prevent the generation of harmful content. We also replicate the results for two sophisticated types of hate campaigns. While the original findings seem to have been overestimated slightly due to bias in the datasets, the overall findings can be confirmed. Finally, we compare text- Moderation against the newer omni-Moderation and find that its robustness against adversarial hate campaigns has improved slightly. By clarifying which detector vulnerabil- ities persist, this study informs the community about the longevity of content moderation measurements.

</details>

### [101] [Exact quantum spin liquids with topological order on maple-leaf and trellis lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04319)
*Li Ern Chern,Roderich Moessner,Claudio Castelnovo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 构建了具有键依赖各向异性相互作用的自旋模型，在maple-leaf和trellis晶格上实现了类似Kitaev蜂窝模型的精确Z2量子自旋液体，并通过数值模拟和解析方法研究了其拓扑相图、激发态及相变行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找支持Z2量子自旋液体的新晶格结构，并理解其拓扑序、激发和相变机制，拓展Kitaev模型的物理内涵。

Method: 构造penta-coordinated晶格上的键依赖各向异性自旋模型，结合 replica exchange 量子蒙特卡洛模拟与解析推导，分析基态通量构型、拓扑不变量（如陈数）以及任意子激发特性。

Result: 发现低温下通量有序态（三角形单元为±π/2，六边形为0，正方形单元为π）；绘制出包含Z2和Ising拓扑序的相图；得到马约拉纳费米子质量项的解析表达式，其消失对应相变；建立了涡旋与两种任意子在二聚体极限下的对应关系；发现不同二聚体极限在无涡旋时可连续连接，但在双涡旋扇区经历费米子能隙闭合。

Conclusion: maple-leaf和trellis晶格可支持丰富的Z2量子自旋液体行为，揭示了拓扑相变、任意子激发与通量有序之间的深刻联系，为探索新型拓扑量子物态提供了新平台。

Abstract: We construct spin models with bond-dependent anisotropic interactions on the penta-coordinated maple-leaf and trellis lattices, which yield exact $\mathbb{Z}_2$ quantum spin liquids akin to the Kitaev honeycomb model. We characterize the resulting ground states by their flux sectors, Chern numbers, and topological excitations. Using replica exchange quantum Monte Carlo simulations, we find that the gauge fluxes are ordered at sufficiently low temperatures such that every unit triangle has $\pm π/2$-flux, where the sign is uniform across the system, and every unit hexagon (square) has $0$-flux ($π$-flux) within the parameter space of interest. We map out the topological phase diagram for each model, which reveals parameter regimes hosting $\mathbb{Z}_2$ and Ising topological orders, and we derive an analytical expression for the mass term of the Majorana fermions, the vanishing of which indicates a transition between these phases. We further establish the correspondence between individual vortices (i.e., flux excitations) and two species of anyons in the dimer limit, overcoming the obstacle faced by degenerate perturbation theory in treating odd-length elementary plaquettes. Interestingly, we find that two dimer limits of the maple-leaf model with distinct assignments of anyon species can be smoothly connected to each other in the vortex-free sector, but they are separated by fermion-gap-closing transitions in certain two-vortex sectors.

</details>


### [102] [Dual Charge-Density-Waves in Two-dimensional DyTe3 and Their Distinct Impacts on Magneto-Transport Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04370)
*Shuvankar Gupta,Yasemin Ozbek,Olajumoke Oluwatobiloba Emmanuel,Maya Bostock,Johnathan Kowalski,Pengpeng Zhang,Xianglin Ke*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在DyTe3中，两种正交的电荷密度波（CDW1和CDW2）在低温下共存，其中CDW2的出现显著增强了磁阻并引发多带非线性霍尔响应，而CDW1无明显磁输运异常，表明两者对电子输运的影响本质不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究电荷密度波（CDWs）存在下电磁输运性质的演化仍不充分，尤其是多个CDW共存时的输运行为尚不清楚。

Method: 通过实验测量DyTe3在低温下两个正交CDW共存时的电子输运特性，特别是磁阻和非线性霍尔效应。

Result: CDW1未表现出明显的磁输运异常，而CDW2导致磁阻显著增强和由多带机制主导的非线性霍尔响应。

Conclusion: DyTe3中的CDW1与CDW2对电子输运具有定性上不同的影响，该体系可作为研究准二维材料中多重CDW输运效应的理想模型。

Abstract: Charge-density-waves (CDWs) are macroscopic quantum states defined by periodic modulations in electronic charge density coupled with lattice distortions. Despite significant research efforts, the evolution of electromagnetic transport properties in the presence of CDWs remains largely unexplored. Here, we report the effects of two orthogonal CDWs (CDW1 and CDW2) coexisting at low-temperature in DyTe3 on its electronic transport properties. Importantly, we find that while no clear magnetotransport anomaly is resolved across CDW1 transition under the present experimental conditions, the emergence of CDW2 drives a drastic enhancement in magnetoresistance and a multiband-governed nonlinear Hall response. These results demonstrate that CDW1 and CDW2 in DyTe3 are fundamentally different and impact its electronic transport in qualitatively distinct ways, establishing DyTe3 as a model system for understanding the transport consequences of multiple CDWs in quasi-two-dimensional materials.

</details>


### [103] [Layer Architecture Shapes Electronic, Magnetic, and Lattice Interactions in Ruddlesden-Popper Nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04532)
*W. He,X. Guo,X. Luo,J. Thomas,J. Sears,Sophia F. R. TenHuisen,Ziqiang Guan,Xinglong Chen,D. A. Dahlbom,B. Zager,J. Pelliciari,Yi-Feng Zhao,H. LaBollita,Hong Zheng,M. K. Lajer,J. F. Mitchell,V. Bisogni,A. S. Botana,M. Mitrano,S. Johnston,M. P. M. Dean*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究通过比较La3Ni2O7的两种多晶型（LNO-1313和LNO-2222）及三层层状化合物La4Ni3O10的电子、磁性和晶格激发，揭示了层状结构对镍酸盐超导相关低能物理的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索层状结构如何影响Ruddlesden-Popper镍酸盐中的电子、磁性和晶格相互作用，进而理解其对超导配对机制的作用。

Method: 采用Ni L3边和O K边共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）技术，对LNO-1313、LNO-2222和La4Ni3O10进行详细的比较研究，并结合有效局域自旋模型分析自旋激发行为。

Result: 发现LNO-1313与La4Ni3O10具有相似的电子结构特征（如较弱的轨道极化、增强的3d8L态、更强的电声耦合），而LNO-2222表现出明显不同的物理特性；在强反铁磁层间耦合下，纠缠二聚体模型比传统自旋波理论更适用于描述其自旋激发。

Conclusion: 层间耦合和电子关联的协同作用在决定材料低能激发中起关键作用，为理解多层镍酸盐超导机制提供了重要的实验依据。

Abstract: The discovery of superconductivity in Ruddlesden-Popper nickelates has raised a central question: how does layer architecture shape the electronic, magnetic, and lattice interactions relevant to pairing? Here, we report a detailed comparative study of the two polymorphs of La3Ni2O7--the alternating monolayer-trilayer (LNO-1313) and bilayer (LNO-2222) structures--and the related trilayer compound La4Ni3O10, using both Ni L3- and O K-edge RIXS. We find that LNO-1313 and La4Ni3O10 share strikingly similar electronic, magnetic, and lattice excitations, whereas bilayer LNO-2222 exhibits distinct features. Compared to LNO-2222, LNO-1313 and La4Ni3O10 have weaker orbital polarization, enhanced 3d8L character, a reduced out-of-plane magnetic-exchange scale, and stronger EPC. Within an effective local-moment framework, an entangled-dimer scenario provides a natural description of the spin excitations generated by strong antiferromagnetic interlayer coupling. Its advantage over conventional spin-wave theory is clearest in bilayer LNO-2222, where the interlayer coupling dominates the intralayer interactions. These findings provide critical experimental constraints for future theoretical models for the low-energy physics relevant to superconductivity in these layered nickelates.

</details>


### [104] [Environment-assisted transport in a strongly correlated boundary-driven Fermi-Hubbard chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05137)
*Hakan Yeler,Özgür Çakır*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了一维Fermi-Hubbard链在粒子库和局域去相位浴作用下的稳态输运，发现去相位诱导的两种不同输运增强机制，源于Hubbard带内和带间重新分布过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索强关联开放系统中相干驱动、去相位与量子Zeno效应之间的相互作用机制。

Method: 使用TEBD方法求解Lindblad主方程，模拟非平衡稳态输运。

Result: 发现电流随边界驱动出现双峰结构；引入去相位后出现两个不同的输运增强区域，分别对应Hubbard带上部内的重新分布和不同Hubbard带间的跃迁。

Conclusion: 多体关联显著影响强关联开放系统中去相位诱导的输运行为，揭示了两种不同的去相位诱导增强机制。

Abstract: We study steady-state transport in a one-dimensional Fermi-Hubbard chain coupled to particle reservoirs at the boundaries and to local dephasing baths at each site, using the time-evolving block decimation (TEBD) method to solve the Lindblad master equation. In the absence of dephasing, the current exhibits two well-separated maxima as a function of the boundary driving rate, reflecting the distinct charge and spin energy scales of the strongly correlated regime. Upon introducing dephasing, we find two distinct dephasing-induced transport-enhancement regimes, in contrast to the single enhancement previously reported for spinless fermions. Analysis of the non-equilibrium steady state in the Hamiltonian eigenbasis reveals that the two regimes originate from distinct dephasing-induced redistribution processes: the first involves redistribution within the uppermost Hubbard band, while the second involves transitions between Hubbard bands. Our results demonstrate how many-body correlations shape the interplay between coherent driving, dephasing, and quantum Zeno physics in strongly correlated open systems.

</details>


### [105] [Dipolar and quadrupolar spin supersolid states in a spin-1 triangular antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05181)
*Yixuan Huang,Yuan Gao,Wei Li,Seiji Yunoki,Sadamichi Maekawa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究利用密度矩阵重正化群方法系统地研究了三角晶格上自旋-1反铁磁海森堡模型在外加磁场下的量子相图，发现了多种具有U(1)对称性和平移对称性破缺的偶极与四极自旋超固态，并揭示了其在实验上可观测的动力学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究三角晶格自旋-1系统中阻挫、各向异性和塞曼相互作用如何共同驱动新型自旋超固态的形成。

Method: 采用密度矩阵重正化群（DMRG）方法，在不同单离子各向异性$D_z$和磁场条件下计算系统的序参量、自旋超流刚度及动力学自旋结构因子，分析有限尺寸效应。

Result: 识别出Y型和V型偶极自旋超固体、四极自旋超固体以及上-上-下相；发现高场下存在四极自旋超流态；观测到Goldstone模和类转子极小等特征激发谱。

Conclusion: 单离子各向异性和磁场可协同调控三角晶格自旋-1系统的量子相变，诱导出多种可由中子散射实验探测的自旋超固态，为理解Na₂BaNi(PO₄)₂等材料中的物性提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We present a systematic numerical study of the spin-1 antiferromagnetic Heisenberg model on the triangular lattice in an out-of-plane magnetic field, using Density Matrix Renormalization Group (DMRG) methods. By mapping out the quantum phase diagram as a function of the single-ion anisotropy $D_z$ and magnetic field, we identify distinct dipolar and quadrupolar spin supersolid states, characterized by spontaneous U(1) symmetry breaking with finite spin superfluid stiffness coexisting with longitudinal translational symmetry breaking. At zero field, the dipolar spin supersolid with a 'Y'-type spin configuration persists down to $D_z = 0$, whereas the quadrupolar spin supersolid prevails at large $D_z$. At intermediate fields, the phase diagram is dominated by an up-up-down phase. At high fields below saturation, a quadrupolar spin superfluid emerges in the large-$D_z$ regime, whereas a dipolar spin supersolid with a 'V'-type spin configuration dominates at small $D_z$. These phases are characterized through their order parameters and spin superfluid stiffness using calculations on various system sizes. Furthermore, the dynamical spin structure factor is obtained across the phase diagram, where characteristic spectral signatures of different phases are observed, including the gapless Goldstone mode and the roton-like minima. These features are directly accessible to inelastic neutron scattering experiments. Our results provide a theoretical understanding of the interplay between frustrations, anisotropy, and Zeeman interactions in driving distinct spin supersolid phases in the spin-1 system, which are relevant to various triangular-lattice antiferromagnets such as Na$_2$BaNi(PO$_4$)$_2$ and K$_2$Ni(SeO$_3$)$_2$.

</details>


### [106] [Breakdown of Anomalous Hall Scaling in Dilute Kondo System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05319)
*Arnab Bhattacharya,Prashant Singh,Ajay Kumar,Afsar Ahmed,Yaroslav Mudryk,I. Das,Anis Biswas*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在非f电子Heusler合金Ru₂Mn₀.₅Ti₀.₅Ge中发现由Kondo杂化诱导的平带与自旋轨道耦合共同作用产生的本征贝里曲率驱动的反常霍尔输运，揭示了平带Kondo体系中拓扑与强关联相互纠缠的新物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索强电子关联与贝里曲率输运的交叉领域，特别是在由平带物理而非传统f电子态引发关联的系统中，理解其对反常霍尔效应的影响。

Method: 结合实验测量（反常霍尔效应随温度的变化）与第一性原理计算，分析贝里曲率来源及其与Kondo相干和平带结构的关系。

Result: 观测到非单调的反常霍尔响应，在磁相变附近违背费米液体标度关系，并在低温下随Kondo相干出现而恢复；理论计算表明贝里曲率源于费米能级处Kondo杂化诱导的平带与自旋轨道耦合反交叉的协同作用。

Conclusion: 平带的Kondo相干显著影响贝里曲率相关的铁性响应，表明在倒易空间拓扑与强关联内在交织的体系中，需建立严格的反常输运理论框架。

Abstract: The confluence of strong electronic correlations and Berry curvature-driven transport constitutes a largely underexplored frontier in quantum materials research, particularly in systems where electronic correlations arise from flat-band physics rather than conventional $f$-electron states. Here, we report an intrinsic Berry curvature driven anomalous Hall transport in the dilute Kondo system Ru$_2$Mn$_{0.5}$Ti$_{0.5}$Ge, a non-$f$-electron Heusler alloy hosting flat bands with van Hove singularity proximate to the Fermi level. The anomalous Hall response is strikingly non-monotonic, violating the Fermi-liquid scaling relation near the magnetic transition and is restored only at low temperature, coincident with the onset of Kondo coherence. \textit{Ab initio} calculations strongly establish that the Berry curvature originates from the Kondo hybridization induced interplay of flat bands and spin-orbit coupling mediated anticrossings at the Fermi level. Our findings demonstrate that Kondo coherence of flat bands bears a profound impact on Berry curvature associated ferroic responses, mandating a rigorous theoretical understanding of anomalous transport in the regime where reciprocal space topology and strong correlations are intrinsically intertwined.

</details>

### [107] [Accessing the Wess-Zumino-Witten term using Lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05006)
*Fernando Romero-López,Stephen R. Sharpe*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了手征微扰理论中的Wess-Zumino-Witten相互作用，推导了有限体积下K\overline K \to π^+ π^0 π^-等过程的理论框架，并给出了可用于格点QCD计算的2和3粒子体系谱的分析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于手征反常的存在，Wess-Zumino-Witten相互作用导致了非零的K\overline K \to π^+ π^0 π^-振幅，但如何在有限体积下提取此类振幅仍需系统研究。

Method: 基于有限体积形式主义，结合格点QCD可计算的两体和三体能谱，构建了适用于K\overline K + 3π（I=0）和πK + ππK（I=1/2, 3/2）系统的理论框架，并推导了相关K矩阵的阈值展开。

Result: 得到了用于有限体积计算的2↔3和3↔3散射过程的K矩阵阈值展开式，并利用手征微扰理论计算了这些展开系数的领头阶预测值。

Conclusion: 该工作为通过格点QCD模拟提取涉及手征反常的过程（如K\overline K \to πππ）提供了可行的理论工具。

Abstract: The Wess-Zumino-Witten interaction in chiral perturbation theory is a manifestation of chiral anomalies. One of its consequences is the presence of a nonvanishing $K \overline K \to π^+ π^0 π^-$ amplitude. We derive the finite-volume formalism that allows one to access this, and related, amplitudes, using the spectra of two and three particles in finite volumes, spectra that can be calculated using lattice QCD. Specifically, we present the formalism for the systems $K \overline K + 3 π$ with $I=0$ and $πK + ππK$ with $I=1/2$ and $3/2$. In addition, we determine the threshold expansions for the $2\leftrightarrow 3$ and $3\leftrightarrow 3$ K matrices that enter the formalism, and calculate the leading order predictions from chiral perturbation theory for the coefficients that enter these expansions.

</details>


### [108] [Equation of state of dense magnetized QCD matter via a robust analytic continuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05108)
*S. Borsányi,B. B. Brandt,G. Endrődi,J. N. Guenther,G. Markó,M. A. Petri,A. D. M. Valois*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本研究利用格点QCD在非零晶格间距下计算了致密且强磁化强相互作用物质的状态方程，采用2+1+1味根化 staggered 夸克并满足物理质量、奇异性中性和同位旋不对称性条件。通过引入虚化学势并应用改进的$T'$-展开方案进行解析延拓，有效减小了系统误差，提高了外推可靠性。结果提供了可用于重离子碰撞现象学的压力、熵密度和相互作用量等热力学量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更准确地描述重离子碰撞中极端条件下的强相互作用物质状态，需在包含磁场和有限密度的情况下计算状态方程，并满足实际物理约束如奇异性中性和同位旋不对称性。传统解析延拓方法存在较大系统误差，亟需改进。

Method: 采用2+1+1味根化 staggered 夸克的格点QCD模拟，在非零磁场和虚化学势下进行计算，并应用适应于磁场环境的$T'$-展开方案实现向实化学势的解析延拓，从而规避符号问题并降低系统误差。

Result: 获得了压力、熵密度和相互作用量等热力学观测量在不同化学势和温度下的数值结果，并以表格形式提供；结果显示$T'$-展开方案相比传统方法显著减小了系统误差，使外推更加可靠。

Conclusion: 改进的$T'$-展开方案能更精确地处理磁化与致密强相互作用物质的状态方程，所得结果具有更高精度和更好的可控性，适用于重离子碰撞中的唯象学建模。

Abstract: We compute the equation of state of dense and magnetized strongly interacting matter using lattice QCD at a non-zero value of the lattice spacing. Our simulations employ $2+1+1$ dynamical flavors of rooted staggered quarks with masses tuned to the physical point. To conform with experimental constraints, we impose strangeness neutrality and isospin asymmetry on our observables. We circumvent the sign problem by simulating imaginary values of the chemical potential and applying the $T'$-expansion scheme to carry out the analytic continuation of our observables to the real chemical potential axis. This novel scheme, adapted to the setup with nonzero magnetic fields, is demonstrated to produce significantly smaller systematic errors in the final observables, compared to the standard analytical continuation at fixed temperature, allowing for better-controlled extrapolations. Our results for the pressure, the entropy density and the interaction measure are tabulated for direct use in heavy-ion phenomenology applications.

</details>
