# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [A Constraint-Aware Generative Framework for Synthetic Origin-Destination Demand in Logistics Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04345)
*Leian Chen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种约束感知的条件生成框架，用于在分层物流网络中生成合成的起讫点需求，能够适应网络拓扑变化并满足操作约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖历史数据，无法根据网络拓扑变化生成适应性的需求模式，且难以满足实际操作约束。

Method: 构建一个条件生成模型，将需求建模为给定起点下的目的地条件分布，并通过可微分约束将操作规则嵌入生成目标中，支持灵活的条件控制和网络配置适应。

Result: 在真实工业物流网络上实验显示，相比图神经网络基线方法提升16%，操作合规率达87%，并具备高效的冷启动适应能力。

Conclusion: 该框架能生成拓扑合理且操作可行的需求数据，适用于运力规划、网络设计评估和路径优化等应用场景。

Abstract: Large-scale logistics networks require synthetic data generation capabilities to support scenario-based planning under novel conditions-such as network reconfiguration and demand shocks. Existing approaches, which rely primarily on historical observations, lack the ability to generate demand patterns that adapt to changes in network topology while respecting operational constraints. We propose a constraint-aware conditional generative framework for synthetic origin-destination demand generation in hierarchical logistics networks. The framework models demand as a conditional distribution over destinations given each origin, enabling topology-aware synthesis that is both topologically realistic and operationally feasible. Operational guidance is incorporated directly into the generative objective via differentiable constraints, while a flexible conditioning mechanism supports various operational contexts and adaptation to evolving network configurations. We instantiate the proposed framework based on a conditional generative model. Experimental validation on industrial real fulfillment and transportation network demonstrates 16% improvement over graph neural network baselines, 87% operational compliance, and efficient cold-start adaptation, enabling applications in capacity planning, network design evaluation, and routing optimization.

</details>


### [110] [Grid-Mode-Aware Model Predictive Control of Hybrid Energy Storage Systems for AI Data Center Power Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04398)
*Xin Chen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种电网模式感知的模型预测控制（G-MPC）框架，用于协调混合储能系统（HESS）以平滑AI数据中心对电网的功率需求，并抑制与脆弱电网振荡模式相关的功率分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对AI数据中心高度可变负载对电网稳定性的影响，需有效抑制由负载引起的电网振荡，提升电网友好性。

Method: 设计了嵌入带通滤波动态的G-MPC框架，通过滚动时域优化协调电池储能（BESS）和超级电容（SC），联合最小化电网侧功率包络、爬坡率、模态功率越限及储能损耗成本，并满足运行约束；采用固定再优化算法实现高效实时求解。

Result: 仿真结果表明该框架在平滑电网功率、抑制特定振荡模式方面有效且灵活，计算效率高；明确抑制脆弱模式相关功率分量比单纯降低总负荷波动更能有效缓解电网振荡。

Conclusion: 所提G-MPC框架能有效集成AI数据中心负载，通过针对性抑制关键电网振荡模式增强系统稳定性，为高变负荷的电网友好接入提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: To facilitate the grid-friendly integration of highly variable AI data center loads, this paper proposes a grid-mode-aware model predictive control (G-MPC) framework for managing a hybrid energy storage system (HESS) to smooth grid-side power demand. The framework optimally coordinates a battery energy storage system (BESS) and a supercapacitor (SC) by solving a multi-step optimization problem in a receding-horizon manner. In particular, band-pass filter dynamics are directly embedded in the G-MPC formulation to extract and suppress grid-side power components associated with vulnerable grid oscillatory modes, thus mitigating load-induced grid oscillations. The resulting G-MPC optimization jointly minimizes violations of grid-side power-envelope, ramp-rate, and modal-power requirements and the degradation and power-ramping costs of the BESS and SC, while satisfying power limits, state-of-charge limits, and other operational constraints. To enable real-time implementation, a fix-and-re-optimize algorithm is developed to solve each G-MPC problem efficiently while preventing simultaneous charging and discharging. Extensive simulations demonstrate the effectiveness, flexibility, and computational efficiency of the proposed framework. The results also highlight the importance of explicitly suppressing power components associated with vulnerable grid modes, rather than merely reducing overall load variations, to effectively mitigate grid oscillations.

</details>


### [111] [Estimating the Health and State of Charge of Each Cell in a Second-Life Battery System from Field Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04487)
*Martin Cornejo,Julian Meyer-Schwickerath,Juan Victor Sandalinas,Andreas Jossen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于高斯过程回归的框架，仅利用运行数据联合估计电池的状态和参数，并重建开路电压曲线，揭示了第二生命电池系统中健康状态异质性和荷电状态不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对二手电池开路电压曲线未知的问题，需要一种可靠的方法来估计其健康状态（SOH）和荷电状态（SOC）。

Method: 采用等效电路模型并结合高斯过程回归方法，仅从现场运行数据中联合估计电池的状态与参数，重建OCV曲线，并在模块层面聚合单元的SOH和SOC进行分析。

Result: 应用于由27个模块和324个电池组成的实际二手电池系统，发现了SOH异质性、系统性SOC失衡以及两个故障电池；集总模块模型无法捕捉限制性电池行为，导致SOH最多高估31%，SOC最多高估23%。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能有效识别二手电池系统中的关键退化特征和不平衡现象，相较于集总模型更准确地反映个体电池差异，对电池管理系统具有重要意义。

Abstract: Effective use of battery storage depends on reliable estimation of its state of health (SOH) and state of charge (SOC). Model-based state estimation requires the open-circuit voltage (OCV) curve, which is typically unknown for second-life batteries. We present a framework that jointly estimates the states and parameters of an equivalent circuit model solely from field operation data, using Gaussian process regression to reconstruct the OCV curve. Applied to a real second-life battery system of 27 modules and 324 cells, it reveals SOH heterogeneity, a systematic SOC imbalance, and two faulty cells, all validated against a reference measurement. We aggregate the cell SOH and SOC to module level and benchmark them against a lumped-module model fitted without the individual cell voltages. The lumped-module model follows the average behavior and cannot capture the limiting cells, overestimating SOH by up to 31% and SOC by up to 23%.

</details>


### [112] [Integrating Agentic Artificial Intelligence with High-Performance Computing for Grid Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04544)
*Samim Konjicija,Slaven Peles*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AgentiGrid的智能体AI框架，结合大语言模型（LLM）与高性能计算（HPC），用于加速多场景潮流分析。该框架能够自主决策、调整参数、调用ExaGO工具进行分析、解释结果并规划下一步操作，支持多种LLM后端，并具备实时可视化和自然语言报告生成功能，可在20次迭代内高效收敛交流最优潮流问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升电力系统多场景潮流研究的效率与自动化水平，克服传统方法在复杂性、灵活性和响应速度上的局限，将大语言模型的推理能力与高性能计算的精确分析相结合。

Method: 构建一个名为AgentiGrid的自主智能体框架，集成多种大语言模型（如OpenAI、Anthropic、Ollama等）与HPC工具ExaGO，通过任务特定提示增强上下文理解；框架可自主提出参数修改、调用确定性、随机性和安全约束下的最优潮流分析，解释结果并决定后续动作，支持交互式搜索引导、目标感知的后分析及并发变体探索，并通过Streamlit实现图形化界面与实时可视化。

Result: AgentiGrid能够在少于20次迭代内自主收敛输电约束下的交流最优潮流问题，具有接近完美的可靠性；实现了高效的多场景潮流优化，支持自然语言报告生成与实时进度监控。

Conclusion: AgentiGrid成功融合了大语言模型的推理能力和高性能计算的精确分析能力，为电力系统的自动化分析提供了可扩展、可靠且用户友好的解决方案，展示了AI代理在工程仿真领域中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: We present AgentiGrid, an agentic artificial intelligence (AI) framework that integrates large language models (LLMs) intelligence and high-performance computing (HPC) to streamline and accelerate the multi-scenario power flow studies. AgentiGrid is an autonomous decision-making agent that proposes parameter modifications, invokes analyses through HPC analysis toolkit ExaGO, interprets results, and determines subsequent actions. ExaGO provides multiple power flow applications that can perform deterministic, stochastic and security constrained optimal power flow analyses. AgentiGrid provides backends to multiple LLMs (OpenAI, Anthropic, Ollama, and Ollama cloud) augmented with context specific and task specific prompts. Key features include interactive mid-search steering, goal-type-aware post-search analysis, and concurrent variant exploration for power flow optimization. A Streamlit-based graphical launcher provides real-time visualization of iteration progress and generates reports in natural language. AgentiGrid is capable of autonomously converging transmission constrained alternating current optimal power flow in under 20 iterations, with near-perfect reliability

</details>


### [113] [Bayesian thermal digital twin for a space habitat subjected to an impact event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04614)
*Sreehari Manikkan,Seungho Rhee,Herta Montoya,Davide Ziviani,Shirley J. Dyke,Ilias Bilionis*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于贝叶斯的热数字孪生模型，用于空间栖息地在微陨石撞击后引发的热异常检测与健康状态估计，结合物理与网络子系统模型，实现对撞击位置、时间与严重程度的实时推断，并支持自主运行决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对空间栖息地中可能由微陨石撞击引起的结构损伤和热异常问题，现有环境控制与生命支持系统（ECLSS）缺乏足够的热态数字孪生支持，难以实现韧性与自主决策。因此需要发展一种能够适应动态变化、具备不确定性感知能力的热数字孪生方法。

Method: 构建了一个耦合的热阻-电容网络模型，分别表示物理与网络热子系统，并嵌入基于物理的激活函数以实现模型选择自动化、自适应及健康状态估计；采用离线贝叶斯校准物理子系统参数，通过实验温度数据进行训练；对于网络子系统，将防护层厚度减少作为撞击敏感参数，固定其他参数后持续进行贝叶斯推断以估计撞击相关参数。

Result: 该框架能够在合成研究中分析超参数选择、可观测性与噪声影响，并利用实验测试台数据验证；结果表明模型可有效检测撞击引发的热异常，以量化不确定性推断撞击相关参数，生成有信息量的温度预测，并支持关键时间估计。

Conclusion: 所提出的贝叶斯热数字孪生框架为未来空间栖息地ECLSS提供了可靠的热状态监控与自主响应能力，增强了系统在突发扰动下的韧性与智能化水平。

Abstract: Space habitats may experience disruptive events, such as micro-meteorite impacts, that can induce structural damage and thermal anomalies in the interior environment, requiring resilient Environmental Control and Life Support Systems (ECLSS). Digital twins offer a promising paradigm for supporting resilience, onboard decision making, and uncertainty-aware autonomy. However, limited work has developed digital twins for the thermal aspects of ECLSS. This work develops a Bayesian thermal digital twin for a habitat cyber-physical testbed experiencing impact-induced thermal anomalies. We construct a coupled thermal resistance-capacitance network model representing the physical and cyber thermal subsystems and embed physics-based activation functions to automate model selection, enable adaptation, and facilitate health-state estimation. Offline Bayesian calibration is performed for the physical subsystem using experimental temperature data. For the cyber subsystem, reduction in structural protective layer thickness is identified as the impact-sensitive parameter. After fixing the physical parameters and insensitive parameters, Bayesian inference is performed continuously to estimate impact-relevant cyber parameters, enabling detection of impact location, timing, and severity. Synthetic studies examine hyperparameter selection, observability, and noise effects, and the final framework is validated using experimental testbed data. Results show that the proposed digital twin can detect impact-induced thermal anomalies, infer impact-relevant parameters with quantified uncertainty, generate informative temperature forecasts, and support time-to-critical estimation for autonomous habitat operation.

</details>


### [114] [On the differential of the exponential map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04762)
*Debanjan Mallik*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了矩阵指数 $B(t) = \mathrm{exp}(A(t))$ 的时间导数，通过泰勒展开、重求和、积分表示以及李括号等工具，建立了导数的统一显式框架，并联系到Gateaux和Fréchet导数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解矩阵指数映射的导数在微分方程、李群理论和控制论中具有重要意义，但其复杂结构需要更清晰和统一的表达方式。

Method: 从泰勒级数展开出发，推导出嵌套求和公式，将其转化为双重求和，并利用欧拉beta函数得到积分表示，进一步用李括号和伴随表示表达；推广到一类问题，并与Gateaux及Fréchet导数建立联系。

Result: 得到了矩阵指数导数的显式双求和与积分表达式，揭示了其与李代数结构的深刻联系，验证了一个已知结果，并证明了Fréchet导数的存在性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个统一且显式的框架来处理矩阵指数映射的导数，增强了对指数映射微分性质的理解，适用于李群与微分几何中的相关问题。

Abstract: We study the time derivative of the matrix exponential $B(t)=\mathrm{exp}(A(t))$, where $A(t)$ is a time-parametrized curve in $\mathrm{Mat}(n)$. Starting from the Taylor series expansion, we derive a nested summation formula, which is then reformulated into a double summation. This expression is converted into an integral representation using Euler's beta function, and further expressed in terms of Lie brackets and the adjoint representation. Later, we extend this to a family of problems and verify a well-known result. Finally, we establish connections to the Gateaux and Fréchet derivatives, proving the latter's existence. These results offer a unified and explicit framework for understanding the derivative of the exponential map.

</details>


### [115] [A Forward Model for Route- and Season-Dependent High-Pressure Compressor Efficiency Deterioration in Turbofan Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05029)
*Yuyou Zhan,Miguel Arana-Catania,Neil Dhir,Yiguang Li*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种前向模拟框架，首次将航线、季节和高度相关的颗粒暴露与高压压气机（HPC）等熵效率损失完整关联，通过四个连续层级预测民用涡扇发动机在实际运行环境下的压气机性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将环境颗粒暴露、叶片沉积和级性能退化视为独立问题，且阶段堆叠方法需预设退化程度而非从运行环境推导，导致从飞行条件到发动机性能损失的因果链不完整。本文旨在建立一个物理可追溯的前向框架，填补这一空白。

Method: 构建一个包含四个层级的前向模拟框架：(1) 飞行阶段解析的HPC入口颗粒剂量计算；(2) HPC各级的沉积分布；(3) 沉积到性能退化的映射；(4) 级热力学堆叠，最终输出整体污染下HPC的等熵效率。以类似CFM56-7B的高涵道比涡扇发动机为基准进行验证。

Result: 模型成功复现了前级主导的沉积特征、初期快速退化并趋于饱和的趋势，以及明显的航线和季节依赖性污染行为；预测的退化幅度和时间尺度与公开的在役维护数据一致。

Conclusion: 该研究首次实现了从操作解析的环境暴露到HPC整体等熵效率损失的完整链条建模，为理解压气机污染机制及优化清洗策略提供了物理一致的预测工具。

Abstract: Civil turbofan engines lose compressor efficiency every time they fly through particle-laden air. Fouling of the high-pressure compressor (HPC) is the dominant recoverable performance-loss mechanism. Existing studies treat environmental particle exposure, blade-row deposition and stage-performance deterioration as separate problems, which limits understanding of the issue. Stage-stacking approaches require deterioration levels to be prescribed rather than derived from the operating environment, leaving the causal chain from route conditions to engine performance unresolved. This paper presents a forward simulation framework that closes this gap by propagating route-, season-, and altitude-dependent particle exposure through four successive layers: (1) flight-phase-resolved HPC inlet dose calculation; (2) stage-wise deposition across the HPC; (3) deposition-to-deterioration mapping; and (4) stage thermodynamic stacking, yielding overall fouled HPC isentropic efficiency. Demonstrated on a representative civil high-bypass turbofan baseline with characteristics similar to the CFM56-7B, the predicted deterioration milestones are confirmed in magnitude and timescale against publicly available in-service support datasets. The results reproduce front-stage-dominated deposition, rapid early deterioration with asymptotic saturation, and clear route- and season-dependent fouling behaviour. To our knowledge, this is the first model that closes the complete chain from operationally resolved environmental exposure to the overall loss of HPC isentropic efficiency in a single physically traceable forward framework.

</details>


### [116] [Robust stabilization with spiking neuronal communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05054)
*Elena Petri,Romain Postoyan,Erik Steur,W. P. M. H.,Heemels*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于脉冲神经网络的鲁棒控制框架，通过整合-放电编码器和突触式解码器实现传感器与控制器之间的脉冲通信，并确保系统在扰动下的实用输入-状态稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索类脑计算在控制系统中的应用潜力，以实现高效、低延迟、抗噪声的稳定控制。

Method: 设计基于 integrate-and-fire 神经元的脉冲编码器和突触式脉冲解码器，结合控制器共同构建闭环系统，分析其在噪声和干扰下的稳定性条件。

Result: 给出了保证闭环系统具备实用输入-状态稳定性的编码器、解码器及控制器设计条件，适用于一类非线性系统和所有可镇定可观测的线性时不变系统。

Conclusion: 该方法为神经形态控制提供了理论基础，仿真验证了其在单连杆机械臂上的有效性。

Abstract: Neuromorphic engineering develops hardware and software systems inspired by biological neurons, with the goal of achieving energy-efficient, low-latency, robust, and adaptive computation, communication and control. Its potential impact on systems and control is significant, as it may enable novel approaches to control and estimation by leveraging brain-inspired computation and communication principles. In this context, we present a framework for the robust stabilization of a plant subject to disturbances when the communication between noisy sensors and the controller relies on spiking signals generated by neuron-inspired schemes. The communication scheme consists of a spike encoder on the sensors side, which is based on integrate-and-fire neurons that convert the analog plant output measurement into a spiking signal, and a spike decoder on the controller side inspired by synaptic processing to convert the received spiking signal into an analog signal. We provide design conditions on the spike decoder, the spike encoder as well as on the controller under which the closed-loop system exhibits a practical input-to-state stability property, where the adjustable parameters are the amplitudes of the spikes. The results are shown to be applicable to a class of nonlinear systems as well as to any stabilizable and detectable linear time-invariant system. Numerical simulations on a single-link manipulator illustrate the potential of the approach.

</details>


### [117] [Regional Frequency Constrained Dispatch Method Considering Spatial-joint Stochastic Disturbances and Contingencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05087)
*Nian Liu,Yubing Chen,Kai Jiang,Jiahao Liu,Cheng Wang,Tianshu Bi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种考虑空间联合随机扰动和故障的区域频率约束调度方法，以应对可再生能源高渗透下的频率稳定性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统频率安全约束调度方法无法准确反映区域频率异质性和空间相关随机扰动，导致频率安全控制不准确。

Method: 构建了基于Vine-Copula的多区域频率响应模型，并采用渐进隐瓶颈物理信息神经网络刻画频率动态特性，进而建立包含CVaR形式区域随机频率约束的日前调度模型。

Result: 在IEEE 118节点系统上的案例研究表明，所提方法相比传统的统一惯性中心方法，区域频率最低点和变化率分别改善了32.76%和2.06%。

Conclusion: 所提出的区域频率约束调度方法能更有效地缓解区域频率越限问题，提升系统频率稳定性。

Abstract: The increasing penetration of renewable energy challenges frequency stability due to high variability and declining inertia. Traditional frequency security constrained dispatch methods fail to capture regional frequency heterogeneity and spatially correlated stochastic disturbances, resulting in inaccurate frequency security enforcement. To address this, a regional frequency constrained dispatch method is proposed, considering the spatially joint stochastic disturbances and contingencies. Firstly, a multi-regional frequency response model is constructed, incorporating the Vine-Copula based characterization of regional stochastic disturbances and regional frequency support. Then, a progressive latent-bottleneck physics-informed neural network is applied to characterize differential frequency nadir terms and regional frequency support integral terms via an encoder-decoder architecture, which enables optimization compatible expressions of system frequency dynamics. Finally, a day ahead dispatch model is developed in which regional stochastic frequency constraints are embedded using a CVaR-based formulation. A case study on the IEEE 118-bus system shows that the proposed method outperforms the unified center of inertia embedded approach in mitigating regional frequency violations, with the regional frequency nadir and RoCoF improved by 32.76% and 2.06%, respectively.

</details>


### [118] [Locational Marginal Pricing for Adaptive Robust Look-Ahead Dispatch with Casual Affine Recourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05156)
*Aidan Looney,Qian Zhang,Le Xie*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于自适应鲁棒前瞻经济调度（LAED）的边际定价机制，以应对净负荷不确定性。通过引入因果仿射响应策略（CAR），解决了传统方法在灵活性定价上的不足，实现了市场出清模型中能量与阻塞价格的分解，并验证了其在不同场景下的经济合理性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有确定性多区间调度在预测误差较大时无法正确反映灵活性价值，而完全自适应鲁棒调度虽能处理不确定性，但因存在多个最坏情况轨迹而导致缺乏统一市场价格。因此需要一种可形成价格且具可行性的调度模型。

Method: 提出因果仿射递归（CAR）作为可实现定价的近似方法，采用因果仿射响应策略替代独立优化的轨迹特定调度，构建保持直流节点边际电价（LMP）能量与阻塞分解结构的市场出清模型，并推导其鲁棒对等式进行求解。

Result: CAR-LMP结合调整后的当前结算方式可支持调度跟随并消除当期机会成本；仿真表明该方法在小规模直流实例、十机组系统和IEEE 300节点系统中表现良好，在无需鲁棒时精确退化为确定性LMP，灵活性稀缺时产生有意义的价格变动，且在宽松爬坡条件下价格稳定。

Conclusion: 所提出的CAR定价机制为自适应鲁棒调度与基于市场的定价之间提供了实用的桥梁，兼具理论合理性与实际应用前景。

Abstract: This paper develops a marginal pricing mechanism for adaptive robust look-ahead economic dispatch (LAED) under net-load uncertainty. In current market practice, deterministic multi-interval dispatch can misprice flexibility when forecast error is large. Fully adaptive robust (FAR) dispatch captures this uncertainty, but competing worst-case trajectories can imply different marginal values of demand, leaving no single price for market settlement. We propose causal affine recourse (CAR) as a tractable, price-forming approximation. CAR replaces independently optimized trajectory-specific schedules with a causal affine response policy, yielding a market-clearing model that retains the standard energy and congestion decomposition of DC Locational Marginal Pricing. We then demonstrate that CAR-LMP together with a ramp-adjusted current settlement supports dispatch-following and eliminates current-period lost opportunity costs. We solve the CAR problem by deriving a robust counterpart and present computational evidence on exact small DC instances, a ten-generator system, and an IEEE 300-bus public-network case. These simulations show that the proposed prices collapse exactly to deterministic LAED-LMP when robustness is inactive, remain stable in loose-ramp regimes, and produce economically meaningful price shifts when flexibility is scarce. The proposed pricing mechanism provides a practical bridge between adaptive robust dispatch and market-based pricing.

</details>


### [119] [Context-Enriched Performance Boosting via Operator Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05158)
*Leonardo Massai,Sebastiano Messina,Nicolas Kirsch,Giancarlo Ferrari-Trecate*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种结构化的因子分解方法，用于上下文增强的多输入性能提升（PB）算子，结合了处理重构干扰的Lp稳定动态模块和依赖于干扰及上下文信号的有界矩阵混合器，在保证闭环稳定性的同时提升了控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的PB框架仅利用干扰信息学习控制策略较为困难，缺乏对上下文信息的有效利用，限制了控制性能的进一步提升。

Method: 引入一种结构化因子分解架构，将Lp稳定的动态模块与一个依赖于干扰和上下文信号的有界矩阵混合器相结合，构建因果且保持Lp稳定性的多输入PB控制器。

Result: 在标准PB假设下，该架构能构造性地保持闭环Lp稳定性；在加权包络干扰域上，证明了该分解对于满足上下文一致包络保持性质的因果算子是充分必要的；数值实验显示其优于多种基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的结构化因子分解方法有效融合了上下文信息，在保持系统稳定性的同时显著提升了控制性能，为PB框架提供了更强的表达能力和实用性。

Abstract: Performance Boosting (PB) is a control framework that, for a pre-stabilized system subject to $\mathcal L_p$ process disturbances, parametrizes the controllers that preserve closed-loop $\mathcal L_p$-stability through a causal $\mathcal L_p$-stable operator mapping reconstructed disturbances to corrective control actions. Although this permits optimization over expressive stability-preserving controllers, learning a desired policy from disturbance information alone can be difficult. We introduce a structured factorization for context-enriched, multi-input PB operators. The proposed architecture combines an $\mathcal L_p$-stable dynamical module that processes reconstructed disturbances with a uniformly bounded matrix-valued mixer depending on disturbances and contextual signals. Under the standard PB assumptions, this factorization preserves closed-loop $\mathcal L_p$-stability by construction. Moreover, on a weighted-envelope disturbance domain, we prove that the factorization is necessary and sufficient for causal operators satisfying a context-uniform envelope-preservation property. A numerical moving-gate navigation experiment demonstrates the advantages of the proposed architecture over context-agnostic PB, MAD, and reference-aware PB baselines.

</details>


### [120] [On the Concept of an Optimal Portfolio of Uncertain Flexible Loads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05176)
*Julie Rousseau,Philipp Heer,Kristina Orehounig,Gabriela Hug*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了在新的市场规则下，如何通过灵活负荷提供备用容量以增强电力系统稳定性，并提出了联合机会约束的备用量化问题的解析重构方法，同时引入了最优灵活负荷组合的概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于灵活负荷的能量容量有限且可用性不确定，传统的备用容量计算方法不再适用，因此需要针对这些特性开发新的模型和方法。

Method: 推导出联合机会约束备用量化问题的两种解析重构形式：一种是在某一方向占主导时的精确重构，另一种是其他情况下的近似重构；并理论分析新增负荷对组合备用容量的边际贡献。

Result: 数值结果表明所提出的解析重构方法与精确解高度一致，平均绝对误差仅为2.5%；案例研究验证了最优负荷分组的存在性，并能预测负荷边际价值最高的组合。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法能够有效处理灵活负荷在备用市场中的不确定性与能量限制，为构建最优负荷组合提供了理论支持和实用工具。

Abstract: Flexible loads can enhance power system stability by providing reserves, but their limited energy capacity and uncertain availability distinguish them from conventional generators. To accommodate these characteristics, the Danish Transmission System Operator (TSO) recently introduced new reserve market rules that incorporate energy constraints and relax reliability requirements. In this context, the optimal reserve quantification becomes a joint chance-constrained reserve quantification problem, which is difficult to solve. In this paper, we derive two analytical reformulations of this problem: an exact one when a reserve direction dominates and an approximate one, otherwise. Furthermore, when flexible loads must collectively satisfy a reliability requirement, we introduce the concept of an optimal portfolio of flexible loads: adding loads with similar expected values but different stochastic behaviors to an existing portfolio may change the total portfolio's reserves. To support this idea, we theoretically study the marginal increase in reserves resulting from adding a load. Numerical results show that our analytical reformulations closely match the exact formulation, with a mean absolute error of 2.5%. Case studies further demonstrate the existence of optimal load groupings and our ability to predict the portfolio in which a load's marginal value is highest, leveraging our theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [121] [Risk-Aware Optimal Control with Rulebooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05199)
*Tichakorn Wongpiromsarn*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于风险感知规则书的多优先级安全关键控制方法，通过字典序优化和分支定界算法求解带不确定性的分层控制问题，并在仿真中验证了算法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在不确定性且具有不同优先级的安全关键控制问题中，传统方法难以有效处理多层次、多风险要求的协调与优化，因此需要一种能够兼顾优先级与风险评估的新型控制框架。

Method: 采用风险感知规则书表示多优先级需求，每个需求关联一个风险度量与可接受阈值；将控制问题建模为对超额风险的字典序优化问题，并设计一种可随时终止的过滤与分支定界算法，在有限计算资源下逐步缩小最优性差距并表征各优先级下的策略集。

Result: 算法能够在任意有限计算预算下提供有效的最优性间隙界，并在额外假设下证明这些间隙随计算资源增加收敛于零；在合成基准（已知最优解）和真实高速公路汇入仿真（基于CVaR的碰撞、刹车、车距和舒适性规则）中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法为处理具有不确定性和优先级结构的安全关键控制问题提供了理论保证与实用算法，支持实时决策与性能验证，适用于自动驾驶等高风险应用场景。

Abstract: We consider safety-critical control problems involving multiple requirements with different priorities and uncertainty in their evaluation. We represent these requirements using risk-aware rulebooks, where each requirement is assigned a risk measure and an acceptable threshold, and a priority relation is defined among the requirements. Each requirement induces a risk-evaluation function that maps a policy to the risk associated with its violation. We formulate risk-aware optimal control with rulebooks as a lexicographic optimization problem over excess risks and develop an anytime filtering and branch-and-bound algorithm that progressively tightens the certified optimality gap while characterizing the corresponding set of policies at each priority level. The algorithm returns a policy together with these gaps, which bound its suboptimality. We prove that these gaps are valid for any finite computational budget and, under additional assumptions, converge to zero as the computational budget increases. We evaluate the algorithm on a synthetic benchmark with a known optimum and a realistic highway-merging simulation with CVaR-based collision, rear-braking, headway, and comfort rules.

</details>


### [122] [Forecast-Ensemble-Based Active Binary-Threshold Query Design for Interval Data Assimilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05307)
*Wataru Hashimoto,Kazumune Hashimoto,Haru Kuroki*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了主动查询设计方法AQD-IDA，用于在区间数据同化中选择信息丰富的不等式查询，提升有限查询预算下的同化精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据同化方法多假设观测为点值，而实际中常获得区间或不等式类粗粒度信息；此外，在观测位置和问题形式均可选但查询次数受限时，缺乏有效机制来主动设计最优观测问题。

Method: 提出AQD-IDA框架，将观测目标和阈值共同作为设计变量，基于预报集合在同化前设计二元阈值查询；构建考虑预报不确定性、查询冗余性和预期后验不确定性减少的查询选择准则。

Result: 在Lorenz-96和球面准地转模型上的实验表明，在固定二元查询预算下，自适应查询设计优于固定或分离设计，能显著提高同化精度。

Conclusion: 主动设计提供给数据同化的信息内容（如选择问什么不等式问题）可大幅提升粗粒度观测的价值。

Abstract: Data assimilation estimates the evolving state of a dynamical system by combining model forecasts with observations. While many conventional methods assume point-valued measurements, practical sensing systems may instead provide coarse information such as binary, ordinal, inequality, or interval-valued reports. Interval Data Assimilation (IDA) provides a principled framework for assimilating such information, but assumes that the observations to be assimilated are specified in advance. In many sensing settings, however, both where to query and what inequality to ask can be chosen, while only a limited number of queries can be issued. This raises a new observation-design problem: how should informative inequality queries be selected from the forecast uncertainty before their responses are known? We propose \emph{Active Query Design for Interval Data Assimilation} (AQD-IDA), which uses the forecast ensemble to design binary threshold queries prior to the IDA update. AQD-IDA treats both the observation target and the threshold defining the inequality as design variables, thereby allowing the assimilation system to determine not only where to observe but also what question to ask. We develop query-selection criteria that account for forecast uncertainty, redundancy among queries, and anticipated reduction in posterior uncertainty. Experiments on Lorenz--96 and a spherical quasi-geostrophic model show that adaptive query design improves assimilation accuracy under a fixed binary-query budget, with joint observation-target--threshold selection outperforming fixed or separately designed queries. These results demonstrate that actively designing the information supplied to data assimilation can substantially increase the value of coarse observations.

</details>


### [123] [Headroom-Aware Stochastic Adaptive Model Predictive Control for Load Frequency Control in Microgrids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05332)
*Erfan Mehdipour Abadi,Shuo Yuan,Le Yi Wang,Caisheng Wang,Feng Lin*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出两种考虑光伏出力随机性的模型预测控制策略（SHCMPC和SAMPC），用于提升高比例逆变型电源微电网中的负荷频率控制性能，有效缓解因可再生能源波动导致的控制饱和问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着逆变型电源在微电网中渗透率的提高，传统基于固定备用裕度的模型预测控制方法在应对短期可再生能源波动时易出现控制与执行器不匹配的问题，导致频率控制性能下降。为此，需要开发能够适应时变、随机调节能力的LFC策略。

Method: 提出了两种考虑裕度感知的MPC策略：一是通过时变输入约束引入预测裕度信息的随机裕度约束MPC（SHCMPC）；二是将裕度意识嵌入目标函数、自适应调整控制惩罚的随机自适应MPC（SAMPC），从而在不依赖硬约束的情况下减少对低裕度单元的依赖。

Result: 仿真结果表明，传统的MPC在可再生出力波动下会出现明显的控制饱和现象，尤其在备用紧张时性能更差。SHCMPC能完全消除饱和事件，SAMPC则在较低计算开销下显著减轻饱和，具有更好的实时应用潜力。

Conclusion: 所提出的SHCMPC和SAMPC策略能有效应对光伏微电网中由可再生能源间歇性引起的控制饱和问题，提升LFC的鲁棒性和可行性，其中SAMPC在计算效率与性能之间取得了良好平衡，适用于实时控制场景。

Abstract: As the penetration of inverter-based resources (IBRs) increases in microgrids, they are increasingly expected to play a greater role in load frequency control (LFC). Model predictive control (MPC) is attractive for LFC because it incorporates system dynamics and operational constraints. However, most MPC-based LFC formulations rely on fixed reserve headroom based on forecasted renewable availability or storage systems. Under short-term renewable intermittency, IBR headroom is stochastic and time-varying, causing optimal control commands to exceed the physically deliverable regulation capability and cause stochastic saturation. This control-actuator mismatch degrades LFC performance. Accordingly, this study develops two headroom-aware strategies for PV-dominated microgrids. First, stochastic headroom constrained MPC (SHCMPC) incorporates headroom predictions through time-varying input constraints to enforce control feasibility. Second, stochastic adaptive MPC (SAMPC) embeds headroom awareness into the MPC objective function by adaptively penalizing control actions based on predicted headroom, reducing reliance on units with limited headroom without hard time-varying constraints. Simulation results show that stochastic saturation degrades conventional MPC-based LFC, particularly under tight reserve margins. Both strategies improve regulation performance. SHCMPC eliminates saturation events, while SAMPC achieves substantial saturation mitigation with lower computational effort, offering a computationally efficient alternative for real-time LFC under stochastic renewable availability.

</details>


### [124] [Data-Driven Generator Transient Prediction for Digital Twin Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05356)
*Emad Sadeghi,Emerson Miller,Kerry Sado,Adel Nasiri*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件条件的Hankel-DMDc模型与校准预测带相结合的发电机数字孪生代理模型，用于在执行前评估有功和无功负荷指令，具有高精度、快速计算和可靠不确定性量化的特点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在负荷切换等瞬态过程中准确预测电压和频率响应，并为操作员提供可信的决策支持，需构建兼顾动态特性与不确定性的高效代理模型。

Method: 采用延迟坐标提升、命令事件记忆特征和事件加权Hankel基构建Hankel-DMDc模型，结合分裂共形校准方法生成电压-频率联合预测区间。

Result: 实验结果显示RMSE为1.058 V和0.155 Hz，90.17%的联合覆盖率，平均带宽为3.55 V和0.566 Hz，50秒开环仿真仅耗时181毫秒，约为实时速度的280倍。

Conclusion: 所提方法实现了高效、精确且具校准不确定性的发电机数字孪生瞬态预测，适用于实时负荷指令评估与决策支持。

Abstract: This paper develops a calibrated transient forecasting surrogate model for generator digital twin (DT) decision support that evaluates planned active- and reactive power load commands before they are applied. The proposed event-conditioned Hankel Dynamic Mode Decomposition with Control (Hankel-DMDc) model combines delay-coordinate lifting, command-event memory features, and an event-weighted Hankel basis so that sparse load-transition dynamics influence the reduced representation and fitted dynamics. This design targets intervals where voltage/frequency deviations and recovery behavior determine whether a candidate load command keeps the system within acceptable limits. To provide operator-facing confidence information, a split-conformal calibration layer is applied to the frozen surrogate model to form event-conditioned joint prediction bands for voltage and frequency. The experimental results show event-window root-mean-square errors of 1.058 V and 0.155 Hz. For a nominal 90% target, the bands attain 90.17% pointwise joint voltage-frequency coverage, with mean band widths of 3.55 V and 0.566 Hz. A 50-s open-loop rollout is computed in 181 ms on a single CPU core, approximately 280 times faster than real time, with forecast accuracy evaluated over horizons up to 5 s. These results demonstrate a computationally efficient advisory framework for generator DTs that combines transient prediction with calibrated uncertainty.

</details>


### [125] [Beyond Scalar Flexibility: From Eligible AI Workloads to Dependable Load Relief](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05406)
*Meiyi Li*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文通过分析155,410个GPU在185天内的运行数据，量化了数据中心在不同时间尺度和可靠性水平下的实际可调度灵活性，发现传统固定百分比假设存在显著偏差，并提出了基于持续时间、可靠性和跨集群组合的更精确灵活性建模方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电网研究通常假设数据中心具有固定比例的灵活负载，但缺乏真实生产数据支持；本文旨在基于真实GPU工作负载轨迹，揭示灵活性随时间、可靠性和集群聚合变化的实际特性。

Method: 利用185天内155,410个GPU的4,439个每小时功率观测数据，构建工作负载语义的灵活性包络；采用蒙特卡洛中位数设施需求基准，计算不同时长（1至24小时）和可靠性水平（如95%可用性）下的可削减负荷，并评估跨集群聚合与调度策略的影响。

Result: 数据中心平均可立即削减负荷为3.55 MW（占工作负载功率12.1%，设施中位功率6.35%）；随着事件持续时间增加，95%可用的缓解能力从1小时2.51 MW降至24小时1.95 MW；固定标量会高估17%-47%或低估/高估特定场景；跨13个集群聚合使四小时坚韧性从0.38提升至0.66，但受协方差限制；调度器几乎未释放额外延迟型容量（仅0.008 MW，95%可用性下为零）。

Conclusion: 数据中心的电网灵活性不应简单表示为固定百分比，而应结合持续时间、可靠性要求、投资组合效应和实现率进行建模，该结果可用于指导并网和需求响应合同的设计。

Abstract: Grid studies often represent data-center flexibility as a fixed percentage of load, although no public production trace has shown how much eligible load persists across event durations or co-moves across clusters. We reconstruct 4,439 hourly power observations from a 185-day trace of 155,410 GPUs and derive a workload-semantic flexibility envelope. The fleet's time-averaged Monte Carlo median facility demand is 55.8 MW, while immediate eligible curtailment averages 3.55 MW after retaining allocated-GPU idle power: 12.1% of workload power and 6.35% of median facility power. Under full realization of that eligibility, 95%-available relief falls from 2.51 MW for one hour to 2.32 MW for four hours and 1.95 MW for 24 hours; a common realizable fraction q scales every value exactly by q. A mean-calibrated scalar overstates these quantities by 17%, 25%, and 47%, while a scalar tail-calibrated at four hours understates the one-hour product by 6% and overstates the 24-hour product by 17%; the share that reproduces the surface varies by a factor of 1.6 across durations and reliability levels. Aggregating 13 clusters raises four-hour firmness from 0.38 to 0.66, but cross-cluster covariance limits the gain. The production scheduler exposes almost no additional delay-based capacity: newly deferrable arrivals average 0.008 MW and have zero 95%-available capacity. These results replace an assumed flexibility percentage with duration, reliability, portfolio, and realizability terms that can be written into interconnection and demand-response contracts.

</details>


<div id='physics.ao-ph'></div>

# physics.ao-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Exploring the role of input data on hail nowcast skill using spatiotemporal neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04427)
*George Pacey,Ophélia Miralles,Ulrich Hamann,Olivia Martius*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 通过改进数据选择和预处理（如增加训练数据量、数据增强和输入时间步），可在不改变深度学习模型架构的情况下显著提升冰雹临近预报性能，其中增加训练年份效果最为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在不改变模型架构的前提下，如何通过调整输入数据的选择和预处理方式来提升深度学习冰雹临近预报模型的性能。

Method: 进行敏感性实验，评估训练数据量、随机数据增强（镜像和旋转）以及输入时间步数对模型性能的影响。

Result: 增加训练数据年份可将预报技巧显著提升，最长提前25分钟；数据增强对大数据集有帮助但对小数据集反而降低性能；输入时间步数的敏感性弱于训练年份的影响。

Conclusion: 优化数据选择和预处理策略可在固定模型架构下带来显著性能增益，为未来深度学习临近预报模型的发展提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Hail can cause large financial losses and poses risks to public safety, making reliable nowcasts essential for timely warnings. Deep-learning approaches have emerged as a strong alternative to conventional methods, but how input data choices affect performance has not been deeply explored. We investigate how the skill of a deep-learning hail nowcasting model can be improved without changing the model architecture. Sensitivity experiments assess the impact of training-data volume, random data augmentation (mirroring and rotation) and the number of input timesteps. Increasing the years of training data substantially improves forecast skill, by up to 25 minutes at later lead times. Augmentation improved performance for larger datasets but interestingly degraded performance for smaller ones. Sensitivity to input timesteps was weaker than sensitivity to training years. These findings show that improvements in data selection and preprocessing can yield substantial gains even with a fixed architecture, offering guidance for future deep-learning nowcasting development.

</details>


### [127] [When a high-mountain slope failure cascades downstream: reconstructing the 26 August 2026 Gyirong mixed rock-ice disaster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04563)
*Rui Li*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 2026年8月26日，中尼边境附近发生高山岩冰崩塌，演变为泥流和洪水级联灾害，影响至吉隆口岸/提姆尔。研究利用多源遥感数据和地形分析重建了灾害全过程，明确了源区、路径及影响范围，并评估了环境背景与观测局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过开放地球观测数据，对跨境高山灾害事件进行完整、可重复且明确包含不确定性的溯源与过程重建，为类似事件提供方法参考。

Method: 结合Sentinel-2、Landsat-9、PlanetScope和Sentinel-1影像，Copernicus DEM地形分析，以及UNOSAT/Copernicus EMS快速制图，采用事件前后对比法和多传感器嵌套包络技术，重建灾害的源-路径-受体系统，并分析气象与热力背景。

Result: 确定崩塌后变化地表最优估计为1.009 km²（范围0.491–1.841 km²），流动路径长达21.843 km，高差约3396 m；未发现明显前期降水触发信号，但7天内气温和正积温突破历史同期极值；快速制图显示下游扰动区达37.353 km²，包含695处建筑损毁点和15.671 km道路受损。

Conclusion: 开放遥感数据可有效约束高山灾害级联过程的一阶特征（如范围、路径、环境背景和影响），但精确的脱离边界、源体积、短时前兆活动、启动机制和局部水文仍难以解析，存在观测盲区。

Abstract: On 26 August 2026, a high-elevation slope failure involving rock and glacier ice near the China--Nepal border developed into a channelised mass-flow and flood cascade that reached Gyirong Port/Timure. Using event-bracketing Sentinel-2, Landsat-9, PlanetScope and Sentinel-1 imagery, Copernicus DEM terrain analysis, and UNOSAT/Copernicus EMS rapid mapping, we reconstructed the source--pathway--receptor system. Nested multisensor envelopes constrained the post-event changed/source surface to a preferred 1.009 km$^2$ (0.491--1.841 km$^2$), while a terrain- and drainage-constrained representative route extended 21.843 km to Gyirong Port and descended about 3396 m. The pre-event Sentinel-2 sequence showed no unambiguous large-scale surface change; the last usable optical image preceded failure by 45.85 h. Neither GPM IMERG V07 Late nor ERA5-Land placed five prespecified precipitation windows in an unusually wet upper tail. In contrast, the 7 d ERA5-Land mean 2 m temperature (9.43 $^\circ$C) and positive-degree-day total (65.94 $^\circ$C d) exceeded all 25 matched years from 2001--2025, providing regional thermal context but not evidence of a trigger. Rapid mapping delineated a 37.353 km$^2$ downstream disturbed footprint containing 695 building points and 15.671 km of roads graded destroyed. These are preliminary mapped features, not a complete damage census. Our main contribution is an uncertainty-explicit, reproducible reconstruction of the full cascade and its observation limits: open Earth observation robustly constrained first-order source extent, propagation, environmental context and impacts, while the exact detached perimeter, source volume, short-lived pre-failure activity, initiation mechanics and source-scale hydrology remained unresolved.

</details>


### [128] [Impact of extremely high temperature on future photovoltaic power potential over East Asia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04632)
*Changyong Park,Ana Juzbasic,Dong-Hyun Cha,Seung-Ki Min,Joong-Bae Ahn,Eun-Chul Chang,Young-Hwa Byun,Youngeun Choi*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了东亚地区极端高温日光伏功率潜力（PVpot）的未来变化，发现随着全球变暖加剧，尤其是在高碳排放情景下，PVpot将在整个东亚地区显著下降，主要受近地面气温升高的驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于全球变暖导致极端高温频发，而光伏发电（PV）是替代化石燃料的重要手段，因此有必要研究极端高温对PVpot的影响，特别是在高排放和气候脆弱区的东亚地区。

Method: 通过多情景气候模拟数据，评估不同未来时期和碳排放情景下东亚地区极端高温日的PVpot变化，并分析关键气候变量对PVpot变化的贡献。

Result: 东亚地区在极端高温日的平均PVpot在未来所有情景和时段均呈下降趋势，且到21世纪末降幅更大，高排放情景下降更显著；在华北和南蒙等光伏热点区域，PVpot最大降幅达-7.2%；与夏季平均条件不同，极端高温日下各子区域PVpot一致减少，近地面气温是主要驱动因素且其影响将随时间增强。

Conclusion: 极端高温会加剧光伏功率潜力的下降，特别是在高碳排放路径下，近地面气温升高成为主导因素，这对未来能源规划和应对气候变化策略具有重要启示。

Abstract: As global warming intensifies, the frequency and intensity of extremely high temperatures are expected to increase. This will impact the production of photovoltaics (PVs), which are increasingly adopted as an effective alternative to replace fossil fuel-based energy sources and reduce CO2 emissions. Furthermore, extremely high temperature days account for a considerable proportion of days with high PV power potential (PVpot). Therefore, this study investigates changes in PVpot on future extremely high temperature days over East Asia, a region with high greenhouse gas emissions and vulnerability to extreme climatic events. The East Asia-averaged PVpot for extremely high temperature days was estimated to decrease across all scenarios and future periods. The East Asia-averaged PVpot for extremely high temperature days was predicted to decrease more substantially toward the late 21st century, with a larger magnitude of decrease expected under the high-carbon emissions scenario compared to the low-carbon emissions scenario. By the mid-and late 21st century, PVpot for extremely high temperature days was projected to decrease in PV hotspot areas, particularly in the regions of northern China and southern Mongolia, by up to -7.2 %. The signs of PVpot projections vary across sub-regions under summer mean conditions, while on extremely high temperature days, PVpot is consistently expected to decrease in all regions. This suggests that extremely high temperatures further intensify the decrease in PVpot. Moreover, under extremely high-temperature conditions, near-surface air temperature has been identified as the primary driver of projected decreases in PVpot among the climate variables considered; its influence is expected to intensify over time, thereby accelerating PVpot decreases under the high-carbon emissions scenario.

</details>


### [129] [Disentangling Internal and Forced Climate Variability with Convolutional Neural Networks using Multivariate Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05359)
*Guillaume Gastineau,Elena Provenzano,Constantin Bône,Nicolas Lebas,Robert C. J. Wills*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 本研究使用U-Net卷积网络从1950年至2022年的多模型数据集中分离气候的强迫与内部变率，验证结果表明其性能优于传统多项式趋势法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确区分气候中的强迫变率和内部变率对于归因气候变化及理解自然气候过程至关重要。

Method: 采用U-Net卷积神经网络，基于五个单模型初始条件大集合的多变量数据集进行训练，并通过留一模型交叉验证评估性能。

Result: 在估算地表气温的强迫变率时，误差为0.1°C至0.4°C，低于外部强迫幅度的一半，且优于四阶多项式趋势法；但在海平面气压和降水等变量上表现较差。

Conclusion: U-Net能有效分离气候强迫信号，性能受限于模型间一致性差和采样不足，未来可通过更大规模多模型数据集提升效果。

Abstract: Long-term climate data exhibit variations composed of internal and forced components. Internal variability arises from natural processes that could occur within a stable climate. Forced variability, on the other hand, reflects climate changes induced, for example, by anthropogenic greenhouse gas and aerosol emissions. Accurately distinguishing between these types of variability is crucial for attributing climate fluctuations and understanding internal variability processes and impacts. In this study, we apply a U-Net convolutional network, a model commonly used in computer vision, to separate forced and internal climate variability from 1950 to 2022 using a multi-model dataset. The dataset includes multiple fields from five single-model initial-condition large ensembles. Cross-validation is conducted by training the U-Net using the data from the ensembles of four models, leaving out the data from one model to assess performance. Validation results yield errors ranging from 0.1°C to 0.4°C for the forced variability of local-scale monthly surface air temperature, which is no more than half the magnitude of external forcing. The U-Net achieves better performance than a simple approach based on a fourth-order polynomial trend for estimating forced variability. The error is mainly due to insufficient sampling and poor agreement among the models, as the U-Net underestimates the warming for the model with the highest transient climate sensitivity during validation. Performance is lower for variables like sea-level pressure and precipitation because of their low ratio of forced to internal variability. This framework might be enhanced by incorporating a larger multi-model dataset in the training and validation.

</details>

### [130] [Publish or Perish: A population-dynamics model of the corruption of peer review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04443)
*Shyam Ranganathan*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过一个迭代博弈的确定性种群动力学模型，探讨了在同行评审中解释者与阐释者之间的不对称动态，揭示了解释者如何在特定条件下逐渐主导评审过程，并分析了制度条件对这一腐败过程是否可逆的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究当同行评审参与者分为阐释者和解释者两组时，评审机制如何从质量控制转变为观点推广，以及这种转变背后的动力学机制。

Method: 采用确定性种群动力学模型模拟“发表或灭亡”迭代博弈，比较固定和幸存者抽取评审小组、不同补充规则、阐释者对解释者的敌意反应、代际比例差异及不同小组规模的影响。

Result: 发现当解释者占比低于 q* ≈ 0.2679 时，解释者可在无阐释者合作的情况下生存并趋向主导；该阈值非普适，外部补充有利于阐释者可消除不可逆点，延迟的代际补充可使阐释者在看似越过阈值后仍恢复。

Conclusion: 模型不仅揭示了同行评审腐败的机制，还明确了决定该腐败是否自我持续或可逆的制度条件。

Abstract: Peer review is commonly treated as a mechanism of quality control. This article examines what happens when participants are split between two groups. Explicators assess whether submissions present logic-based arguments (whether deductive, inductive or abductive), independently of whether they agree with the reasons or the conclusion. Interpreters assess submissions according to their outlook. Their employment of logic is opportunistic, as a means of presenting their own views, but not as a means of assessing others' views. Using a deterministic population-dynamics model of iterative games of "Publish or Perish," this article shows how this asymmetry permits interpreters to increase their population share even when they begin as a small minority. It compares fixed and survivor-drawn reviewer panels, alternative replenishment rules, hostile explicator responses, generational differences in ratios, and different panel sizes. Under survivor-drawn review, explicator hostility to interpreters produces an unstable equilibrium at an explicator share of $q^* = 2 - \sqrt{3} \approx 0.2679$: below this threshold, interpreters can survive without explicator cooperation and continue toward dominance. The threshold is not universal, however. Exogenous replenishment in favour of explicators can eliminate the point of no return, and sufficiently lagged, generational replenishment can permit explicators to recover after it has apparently been crossed. The model therefore identifies not only a mechanism by which peer review becomes corrupted, but also the institutional conditions determining whether that corruption becomes self-sustaining or reversible.

</details>


### [131] [Crowding controls the scaling of bus frequency with demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04520)
*Siddharth Patwardhan,Şirag Erkol,Filippo Radicchi,Marc Barthelemy*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 研究发现城市公交系统中服务频率、乘客需求和路线时长之间存在一个稳健的标度律 f ~ (d/t)^α，指数 α 在 1/2 到 2/3 之间，源于在固定运营预算下最小化乘客总等待时间的优化原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解城市在资源有限且系统状态不确定的情况下如何有效分配交通资源，揭示公交系统运行中的普适规律。

Method: 分析全球19个大都市区约3000条公交线路、每年超过40亿乘客的数据，结合频率与拥挤程度建立优化模型。

Result: 发现了服务频率与需求和路线时长之间的标度律，识别出频率主导（α=1/2）和容量主导（α=2/3）两种普适机制，并发现投资效益在不同城市间差异显著。

Conclusion: 城市公交资源配置遵循简单的优化原则，可归入受约束的资源分配问题范畴，其标度律反映了复杂交通系统中系统性指数的生成机制。

Abstract: Cities must allocate limited resources to maintain mobility, with uncertainties about the resulting state of the system. Analyzing roughly 3,000 bus routes with more than 4 billion yearly riders across 19 metropolitan areas worldwide, we uncover a robust scaling law of the form $f \sim (d/t)^α$ with exponent $α\in [1/2,\,2/3]$, linking the service frequency $f$ to passenger demand $d$ and route duration $t$. We show that this scaling emerges from a simple optimization principle: cities implicitly minimize total passenger waiting time under a fixed operational budget when both schedule frequency and crowding are taken into account. This mechanism produces two universal regimes: a frequency-dominated regime with $α= 1/2$ when crowding is negligible, and a capacity-dominated regime with $α= 2/3$ when most routes are overloaded. Intermediate exponents arise when only part of the network operates near capacity. Furthermore, we find that the benefits of additional investment are highly uneven across systems. For instance, our model suggests that a $20\%$ budget increase yields nearly a 5-minute reduction in daily waiting time per passenger in Boston, compared to only about 1 minute in Paris. These findings place urban transit within a broader class of constrained capacity-allocation problems, while highlighting a distinct regime in which prescribed route demands shape the allocation of limited service resources. The resulting scaling laws show how simple optimization principles can generate systematic exponents in complex transport systems, beyond the dissipation-based frameworks usually considered in physical and biological flow networks.

</details>


### [132] [Low-Dimensional Phase Diagram of Higher-Order Networked Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04692)
*Jia-Jie Qin,Jack Murdoch Moore,Xiaozhu Zhang,Gang Yan*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种降维分析框架，用于揭示高阶网络中临界转变的特性，包括跃迁边界、双稳态区域和相变性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统成对模型无法捕捉高阶网络中的复杂临界现象，需要新的理论工具来理解高阶相互作用对系统动力学的影响。

Method: 发展了一个将高阶网络动力学映射到有效低维系统的解析降维框架，并应用于多种动力学过程。

Result: 准确预测了 tipping 边界、双稳态区域和相变连续性；发现成对与高阶连接之间的一致性（同配混合）增强向激活状态的跃迁，影响系统韧性。

Conclusion: 建立了理解高阶网络中临界转变的通用理论，为预测和管理复杂系统的系统性风险提供了新视角。

Abstract: Higher-order networks exhibit rich critical phenomena that cannot be captured by traditional pairwise models. Here, we develop an analytical dimension-reduction framework that maps higher-order networked dynamics onto an effective low-dimensional system, allowing accurate prediction of tipping boundaries, bistability regions, and the nature of phase transitions. We demonstrate the power of this framework across a range of dynamical processes, revealing distinct effects of higher-order interactions on transition continuity and hysteresis. Furthermore, we find that system resilience exhibits a profound dependence on the alignment between pairwise and higher-order connectivity, with assortative mixing enhancing tipping toward active states. Our findings establish a general theory for understanding the critical transitions in higher-order networks, offering new insights for anticipating and managing systemic risk in complex systems.

</details>


### [133] [Mitigating Disease Spread by Design in Refugee and IDP Camps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05342)
*Giulia Zarpellon,Joseph Aylett-Bullock,Frank Krauss,Miguel Luengo-Oroz*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种方法论，探讨难民营布局对疾病传播的缓解作用，并利用JUNE基于代理的流行病模型进行概念验证实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 难民营和流离失所者营地中疾病传播日益严重，营地布局可能显著影响人群流动与互动，从而影响疾病传播。

Method: 采用JUNE基于代理的流行病模型，进行不同营地布局下的模拟实验，分析其对疾病传播的影响。

Result: 证明了营地布局作为干预因素的潜力，能够为疫情控制提供可操作的见解。

Conclusion: 合理的营地布局设计可作为减缓疾病传播的有效策略，该方法为未来研究提供了框架。

Abstract: Disease spread represents an increasing challenge in refugee and internally displaced person (IDP) settlements. The movement and interaction of people within camps is influenced by their layout, which therefore has the potential to significantly affect disease spread. This work aims at creating a methodology to explore the potential effects of different camp layouts as mitigating factors in the spread of diseases within settlements. We showcase proof-of-concept experiments by leveraging the JUNE agent-based epidemic model, discuss the kind of operational insights this methodology can facilitate, and provide a framework for future investigations.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Fukui-Kawakami chains: spectrum and hidden $\mathfrak{gl}(1|1)$-symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04378)
*Rob Klabbers,Antoine Lefebvre*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了Fukui-Kawakami（FK）自旋链，通过引入扭曲边界条件推广Haldane-Shastry模型，揭示其部分谱可用扭曲Bethe方程描述，并发现额外隐藏对称性；特别在反对称边界条件下，该模型与具有隐含$\mathfrak{gl}(1|1)$对称性的超对称长程模型等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解扭曲边界条件下Haldane-Shastry自旋链的谱结构及其对称性，解释为何部分态可用扭曲Bethe方程描述，并探索可能存在的新对称性。

Method: 利用FK链与原始HS链之间的直接联系，分析其能谱随扭曲参数的变化；结合Yangian最高权态和组合标记‘motifs’描述系统态；在特殊情况下建立与已知超对称模型的对应。

Result: 发现部分能级可通过扭曲Bethe方程和motifs描述，但也存在非Bethe类型态，其能量表现为两个单粒子能量之和，暗示新的隐藏对称性；在反对称边界下，FK链等价于一个具有$\mathfrak{gl}(1|1)$对称性的模型。

Conclusion: FK链保留了HS链的部分可积结构，但引入了更丰富的谱特征和潜在的新对称性，特别是在反对称边界条件下展现出与超对称代数的深刻联系。

Abstract: Fukui and Kawakami showed that the trigonometric Haldane--Shastry (HS) spin chain can be deformed by introducing twisted boundary conditions. In this work we revisit the resulting twisted HS chains, which we call Fukui--Kawakami (FK) chains.
  We analyse their spectra in dependence of the twist parameter, utilising a direct connection with the (untwisted) HS chain. This allows us to explain why part of the spectrum can be described by Fukui and Kawakami's twisted Bethe equations, and can be constructed from Yangian highest weight states. These states can be labelled by combinatorial data called `motifs', as in the HS chain, which cover some of the (deformed) descendants. We furthermore show that there are other descendants which do not follow from these Bethe equations, but whose energy can be described as a sum of two single-particle energies, suggesting additional hidden symmetries.
  We then focus on the special case of antiperiodic boundary conditions, and show that this chain coincides with the `minimally polarised' long-range model recently introduced by Basu-Mallick, Finkel, and González-López. Remarkably, this connection implies that the antiperiodic chain has a hidden $\mathfrak{gl}(1|1)$-symmetry, which we use to relate the HS motifs to their `supersymmetric' $\mathfrak{gl}(1|1)$ counterparts.

</details>


### [135] [Breakdown of the Overdamped Approximation in Fluctuating Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04668)
*Kazuki Fukutani,Takuma Akimoto*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究在时间波动摩擦系数下，过阻尼近似描述布朗运动的有效性，发现环境涨落与惯性消除不交换，快速涨落环境下惯性效应可能增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在摩擦系数随时间波动的复杂环境中，过阻尼近似是否仍适用于描述布朗运动。

Method: 通过比较满足相同涨落-耗散关系的欠阻尼与过阻尼朗之万动力学，分析不同时间尺度下的有效扩散系数，并针对两态马尔可夫摩擦和对数Ornstein-Uhlenbeck环境进行精确求解。

Result: 发现当环境涨落快于速度弛豫时，欠阻尼动力学由摩擦系数的算术平均决定，有效扩散为Deff^under = kB T / ⟨γ⟩；而过阻尼结果为Deff^over = kB T ⟨γ⁻¹⟩；二者在快速涨落下不一致，存在由速度弛豫与环境切换竞争控制的 crossover。

Conclusion: 环境平均与惯性消除操作不可交换，提出了判断过阻尼近似有效性的时标依赖准则，并表明快速环境涨落可能增强而非抑制惯性效应。

Abstract: The overdamped approximation is widely used to describe Brownian motion in complex environments, but its validity becomes nontrivial when the friction coefficient itself fluctuates in time. We investigate this problem by comparing underdamped and overdamped Langevin dynamics subject to the same fluctuating friction and satisfying the fluctuation--dissipation relation at a common temperature. We show that environmental averaging and inertial elimination generally lead to different long-time transport when environmental fluctuations are fast compared with velocity relaxation. For rapidly fluctuating friction, the underdamped dynamics is governed by the arithmetic mean friction and yields $D_{\rm eff}^{\rm under}=k_{\rm B}T/\langleγ\rangle$, whereas the overdamped dynamics gives $D_{\rm eff}^{\rm over}=k_{\rm B}T\langleγ^{-1}\rangle$. For a two-state Markov friction, we derive the finite-time effective diffusion coefficient exactly and identify the full crossover between these two regimes, controlled by the competition between velocity relaxation and environmental switching. We further establish the fast-fluctuation result for general stationary friction processes and verify it for a continuous log-Ornstein--Uhlenbeck environment, for which the characteristic crossover time can also be determined independently. Our results reveal a noncommutativity between environmental averaging and inertial elimination, establish a timescale-dependent criterion for the validity of overdamped dynamics in temporally heterogeneous environments, and show that rapid environmental fluctuations can enhance, rather than suppress, the consequences of inertia.

</details>


### [136] [Mean-field theory for quantum spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04717)
*Mylène Martirosyan,Astrid Monin-Baroille,Leïla Moueddene,Mohammed M. Shabat,Bertrand Berche*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文探讨了平均场理论在量子相变中的应用，特别是针对伊辛模型和Blume-Capel模型，旨在澄清这一较少见领域的均场方法使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清平均场理论在零温下量子相变中应用的局限性和可行性。

Method: 通过回顾平均场理论的发展及其在经典相变中的成功应用，分析其在量子相变中的适用性。

Result: 展示了平均场理论如何被用于理解量子相变，并讨论了忽略热涨落的影响。

Conclusion: 平均场理论虽然主要应用于经典相变，但在适当条件下也可用于研究量子相变。

Abstract: Mean-field theory is a widely used approximation for describing phase transitions, particularly effective above the upper critical dimension. Its origins can be traced back to the van der Waals theory of the liquid-gas transition and Weiss's molecular field theory of the paramagnetic-ferromagnetic transition. However, it was Lev D. Landau who provided a unifying and general framework applicable to a broad class of physical systems. The mean-field approach typically involves neglecting thermal fluctuations, which is a reasonable assumption in many classical contexts. However, its application to quantum phase transitions at zero temperature is less common. The aim of this short pedagogical paper is to explore and clarify the use of the mean-field approach in the less familiar domain of quantum phase transitions. We specifically consider the Ising model and the Blume-Capel model.

</details>


### [137] [Thermodynamic learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04732)
*F. Corberi,S. dello Russo,G. Messuti,S. Scarpetta,L. Smaldone*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过施加外部场训练热力学系统（如伊辛模型）进行记忆和泛化的方法，无需引入额外算法规则，且小系统已表现出良好的学习能力并随规模提升而改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在传统统计力学框架下，利用外部场作为数据来训练完全由哈密顿量决定的热力学系统执行机器学习任务的可能性。

Method: 通过施加适合的外部场模拟训练过程，将这些场视为输入数据，基于伊辛模型与退火二元耦合进行理论分析。

Result: 系统展现出优秀的记忆能力和良好的泛化性能，即使在小规模系统中也表现良好，并且性能随系统尺寸增加而提高。

Conclusion: 热力学系统在纯物理规则下可通过外部场实现类机器学习功能，为理解学习现象提供了新的物理视角。

Abstract: We discuss the possibility to train a thermodynamic system, whose micro-variables are fully determined through the Hamiltonian by the usual statistical mechanical rules, to perform tasks such as memorization and generalization. Training is achieved by the application of suitable external fields, playing the role of {\it data}. At variance with conventional machine learning, no other logical or algorithmic rules are introduced. The system is amenable, in principle, to exact analytical calculations. We specialize this general approach to a prototypical Ising system with annealed dichotomous couplings and study its learning ability. Results indicate excellent memorization and good generalization capacity already for small systems, and a tendency to improve with system size.

</details>


### [138] [Scaling laws and energy dissipation in the dynamics of SLE curve as a 1D turbulence model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04719)
*Yusuke K. Shibasaki*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本研究提出将随机Loewner演化（SLE）作为一维湍流模型，并通过时间坐标变换使其扩散过程满足湍流的Richardson定律和Kolmogorov 4/5定律，进一步建立了对扩散参数κ和Loewner熵的约束条件，并通过数值模拟验证了该模型的标度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索SLE在描述一维湍流中的适用性，建立与经典湍流理论（如Richardson定律和Kolmogorov 4/5定律）的一致性。

Method: 通过时间坐标变换分析SLE的扩散过程x(t)，推导其满足湍流标度律的条件，并引入Loewner熵进行数学分析，辅以数值模拟验证模型特性。

Result: 发现适当的时间变换可使SLE扩散过程满足Richardson定律；推导出使x(t)满足Kolmogorov 4/5定律的κ参数条件；该条件可表述为对Loewner熵S_Loew的约束；数值结果支持模型的标度行为。

Conclusion: 标准SLE在引入合适的时间坐标变换并施加κ和S_Loew的条件下，可成为有效的一维湍流模型。

Abstract: In this study, we demonstrate the characteristics of the stochastic Loewner evolution (SLE) as a one-dimensional (1D) turbulence model. First, we show that the diffusion process $x(t)$ obtained by the time coordinate change in the SLE becomes the time dependent diffusion process that satisfies Richardson's law of turbulence.Subsequently, we derive the condition on the diffusivity parameter $κ$ such that the diffusion process $x(t)$ satisfies Kolmogorov's 4/5th law, which determines the relation between the mean energy dissipation and velocity in the particles of turbulence. Further mathematical analysis showed that this condition is seen as the condition on Loewner entropy $S_{\mathrm{Loew}}$, which includes the white Gaussian noise term $W_{s}$ in the Loewner driving function of the present model. In addition, the numerical simulations were performed to verify the scaling relations of this 1D turbulence model. These results suggest that the standard SLE becomes a beneficial model of 1D turbulence by introducing an appropriate time coordinate change and imposing the conditions on $κ$ and $S_{\mathrm{Loew}}$.

</details>


### [139] [Universal Thermodynamic Law Governing Stochastic Pendulum Clocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04957)
*Yuki Izumida*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于随机摆钟的通用热力学原理，揭示了摆钟相位不确定性与熵产生之间的关系仅依赖于非线性程度，超越了传统热力学不确定关系的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热力学不确定关系（TUR）在欠阻尼系统（如随机摆钟）中失效，因此需要建立新的理论框架来描述此类系统的精度与耗散之间的关系。

Method: 通过将随机摆钟建模为弱非线性振子，推导出其相位不确定性与熵产生之间不确定性乘积的普适表达式，并利用多个代表性摆钟模型验证该公式的有效性与适用范围。

Result: 发现不确定性乘积具有普适且简单的形式，仅取决于系统的非线性程度；该结果在多种摆钟模型中得到验证。

Conclusion: 提出了超越传统TUR的普适热力学原理，为在随机环境中高效运行的最优摆钟设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Clocks' performance, especially their precision, is fundamentally limited by thermodynamic laws as they are physical devices. Yet, it is known that the established thermodynamic uncertainty relation (TUR), which imposes an upper bound on the uncertainty product of the precision of oscillations and entropy production, is violated for underdamped systems such as stochastic pendulum clocks. Here, we show that for a general class of stochastic pendulum clocks described as a weakly nonlinear oscillator the uncertainty product of the phase of a pendulum clock and entropy production takes a universal and simple form that depends solely on the degree of nonlinearity. Its validity and limitations are examined using several representative models of pendulum clocks. Our framework reveals a universal thermodynamic principle governing stochastic pendulum clocks beyond the conventional TUR and provides a foundation for designing optimal pendulum clocks that operate efficiently in stochastic environments.

</details>


### [140] [Anomalous diffusion in porous fractal media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04992)
*Alexander Iomin,Trifce Sandev*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出了一种在分形海绵结构内部的扩散过程模型，该模型推广了梳状结构和分形网格上的扩散过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了描述多孔分形介质中的反常热输运现象，研究者试图建立一个能反映介质分形结构影响的扩散模型。

Method: 将海绵模型视为康托集的直积，并推导出相应的一维扩散过程所遵循的广义福克-普朗克方程，其中包含幂律记忆核和位置依赖的扩散系数。

Result: 分形结构会引起输运系统中的记忆效应和非均匀性，扩散过程由具有幂律记忆核和位置依赖扩散系数的广义福克-普朗克方程控制。

Conclusion: 该模型可用于描述多孔分形介质中的反常热输运，为理解复杂介质中的输运行为提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We suggest a model of a diffusive process inside a fractal sponge structure, which is a generalization of the diffusion processes on a comb and fractal mesh structure. The sponge model is considered as the direct product of Cantor sets. It is shown that the corresponding one-dimensional diffusion process is governed by a generalized Fokker-Planck equation with a power-law memory kernel and a position-dependent diffusion coefficient. That is, the fractal structure of the medium induces memory effects and heterogeneity in the transport system. The considered model may be of interest to describe anomalous heat transport in porous fractal media.

</details>


### [141] [Tensor-Network Inference in a Field-Coupled XY Model for Portfolio Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05045)
*Kartikeya Chowdhry,G Subrahmanya V. R. K. Rao*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 将张量网络方法应用于长仓投资组合构建的场耦合XY模型，通过金融时间序列到网络调整配置的可行路径进行研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用张量网络方法改进长期仅多头投资组合构建的有效途径，结合金融数据特性设计更优资产配置模型。

Method: 采用相关性距离、四点Gromov双曲性和Ward聚类构建稀疏交互路径，利用傅里叶-贝塞尔展开将连续角度配分函数映射为有限电流张量网络，并通过上下行收缩获得单站点边际和平衡余弦得分，最后用softmax映射生成投资组合权重。

Result: 在五个股票市场中研究了不同逆温度beta和集中度gamma参数下的表现，与长期Markowitz有效前沿和标准基准进行了同样本比较，验证了该方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种从金融时间序列到网络调整分配的可处理路径，为长仓投资组合构建提供了新的张量网络框架。

Abstract: We apply tensor-network methods to a field-coupled XY model for long-only portfolio construction. Daily return statistics set asset-specific fields and correlation couplings. Correlation distance, four-point Gromov hyperbolicity, and Ward clustering are used to construct a sparse interaction path. A Fourier-Bessel expansion maps the continuous angular partition function to a finite-current tensor network; bottom-up and top-down contractions then give the one-site marginals and equilibrium cosine scores. A softmax map converts these scores into positive, fully invested portfolio weights. We study five equity markets over continuous ranges of inverse temperature beta and concentration gamma, and compare selected parameter pairs with long-only Markowitz frontiers and standard benchmarks. The same-sample comparisons demonstrate a tractable route from financial time series to network-adjusted allocations; predictive trading performance is outside their scope.

</details>


### [142] [On the growth of operator entanglement in brickwork circuits with Yang--Baxter gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05121)
*Balázs Pozsgay*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了一维砖砌电路中满足辫子关系的局部算符的算符纠缠，针对不同类型的Yang-Baxter门建立了算符Schmidt秩的时间演化上界，并发现了某些情况下存在指数增长的例子，但一般情况下的纠缠增长仍开放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在满足Yang-Baxter关系的门作用下，局部算符的纠缠如何随时间演化，特别是算符纠缠的增长行为及其对量子动力学复杂性的影响。

Method: 通过分类分析满足Yang-Baxter关系的不同类型两体门（如置换门、对合酉门等），利用代数结构和算符Schmidt分解推导其算符纠缠的上界，并构造反例展示可能的指数增长行为。

Result: 证明了对于所有qubit Yang-Baxter门及来自非退化Yang-Baxter映射的置换门，算符Schmidt秩在时间上一致有界；对于对合对偶酉门和置换门的相位修饰情形，最多多项式增长；同时构造出一个七态对合Yang-Baxter门使某单点算符的Schmidt秩指数增长。

Conclusion: 尽管部分结构化的Yang-Baxter门导致算符纠缠保持常数或对数增长，但存在例外情况可能呈现指数增长，因此一般情形下的纠缠演化仍待解决。

Abstract: We study the operator entanglement of local operators in one-dimensional brickwork circuits whose two-site gate satisfies the braid relation; throughout this work, we call such a gate a Yang--Baxter gate. We establish upper bounds for several structured, overlapping classes of Yang--Baxter gates. We show that the operator Schmidt rank remains uniformly bounded in time for all qubit Yang--Baxter gates and, in arbitrary local dimension, for permutation gates obtained from non-degenerate Yang--Baxter maps. We also show that it grows at most polynomially for involutive dual-unitary Yang--Baxter gates and for arbitrary phase dressings of permutation gates obtained from non-degenerate Yang--Baxter maps. These results imply, respectively, constant and logarithmic upper bounds on the operator entanglement. Conversely, we construct a seven-state involutive Yang--Baxter gate without dual unitarity and a one-site operator whose exact operator Schmidt rank grows exponentially, although the corresponding operator entropies remain undetermined. Entanglement growth in the general Yang--Baxter case remains open. All proofs and selected examples were constructed by ChatGPT 5.6 Sol.

</details>


### [143] [Markov chains at the onset of non-reversibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05183)
*Gustave Robichon,Cecile Monthus,Werner Krauth*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了一维路径图及其提升图上可逆马尔可夫链如何被扰动并逐渐转变为非可逆过程，分析了不同稳态下的特征值谱和提速效果，并通过格林矩阵方法计算了Kemeny时间和平均首达时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索可逆马尔可夫链在图结构上的非可逆化扰动机制及其对收敛速度的影响。

Method: 利用特征值分析和格林矩阵方法，在一维路径图与提升图上比较可逆与非可逆马尔可夫链的谱性质和首达时间。

Result: 展示了不同稳态下非可逆链可能具有实特征值或共轭复特征值，揭示了提升图可实现加速，并提供了Kemeny时间和平均首达时间的计算结果。

Conclusion: 非可逆扰动和图的提升结构能显著影响马尔可夫链的动力学特性，格林矩阵方法为分析特征时间提供了有效工具。

Abstract: For a one-dimensional path graph and a lifted path graph constructed from a duplication of each of its sites, we study how a reversible Markov chain can be perturbed and gradually driven into non-reversibility. The reversible Markov chain has a transition matrix that is diagonalizable and features real-valued eigenvalues and eigenvectors. The left and right eigenvectors form a biorthogonal system. We discuss in concrete examples how the transition matrix of a non-reversible Markov chain may be diagonalizable or non-diagonalizable, and it may have real eigenvalues and complex-conjugate pairs. For a number of steady states (flat, square-wave, wedge, V-shape), we compute eigenvalue spectra on both graphs and discuss the speedup that can be achieved through lifting. We develop a Green's matrix formalism, which we use to compute Kemeny times and mean first-passage times, and which provides valuable information and allows us to interpret the results for the characteristic times.

</details>


### [144] [Diffusion under competing bulk and surface stopping mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05247)
*Yilin Ye*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了有界域中受两种独立竞争停止机制影响的反射扩散：体相寿命和表面反应，推导出停止时间与边界局部时间的统计特性，并验证了累积风险服从单位速率指数分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在存在体相衰变和表面反应两种竞争机制下的反射扩散过程的统计行为。

Method: 通过分析停止时间T和边界局部时间L的联合拉普拉斯变换、混合矩以及分裂概率φ，结合Robin-Laplacian和广义Steklov谱理论进行推导，并在三维球体中给出显式结果，辅以蒙特卡洛模拟验证。

Result: 得到了T和L的边缘分布、联合拉普拉斯变换和完整混合矩；证明了p⟨T⟩+q⟨L⟩=1且累积风险pT+qL服从参数为1的指数分布；给出了分裂概率φ的多种等价表达形式；在三维球体情形获得显式解并用模拟验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 两种竞争机制下的反射扩散具有普适的累积风险指数分布规律，分裂概率φ可由几何和频谱特征刻画，理论结果在具体几何中得到验证。

Abstract: We investigate reflected diffusion in a bounded domain subject to two independent, competing stopping mechanisms: an exponentially distributed bulk lifetime of rate $p$ and a surface reaction triggered when the boundary local time exceeds an independent exponential threshold of rate $q$. Denoting by $T$ the stopping time and by $L$ the acquired boundary local time at stopping, we derive their marginal distributions, joint Laplace transform, and complete hierarchy of mixed moments. These statistics are determined by the splitting probability $φ$ that surface reaction occurs before bulk decay. In particular, we establish the identity $p\expect{T}+q\expect{L}=1$ and show that the cumulative risk $pT+qL$ is exponentially distributed with unit rate. We further obtain equivalent representations of $φ$ in terms of the Robin-Laplacian and the generalized Steklov spectra. Explicit results for a three-dimensional ball reveal how competing rates $p,q$ control $φ$ and the $(T,L)$ statistics. Monte Carlo simulations test the universal cumulative-risk law.

</details>


### [145] [Non-reciprocally interacting Ornstein-Uhlenbeck processes: Exceptional points, Anomalous relaxation, Pseudo-equilibrium and Boundary refrigeration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05391)
*Soumya Kanti Pal,Shamik Gupta*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类具有可调非互易性参数 $g$ 的非互易Ornstein-Uhlenbeck（NROU）模型体系，在特殊点 $g=g^*$ 处出现不同阶数的例外点（EP），导致本征值与本征向量合并，并影响自相关与协方差函数的动力学行为。该体系涵盖二聚体、无序系统及映射到Hatano-Nelson模型的多体链。在EP处，弛豫过程被时间多项式修正；在完全非对称极限下，系统呈现‘伪平衡’态且多粒子系统可分解为独立复OU过程。利用Harada-Sasa关系，得到了稳态热耗散的解析表达式，并发现边界制冷效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非互易相互作用如何驱动系统远离平衡态，并探索在非厄米系统中例外点（Exceptional Points, EPs）对动力学和统计性质的影响，特别是在活性物质、生物系统和无序系统中的普遍现象。

Method: 构建一类具有可调非互易参数 $g$ 的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck模型体系，分析其在例外点 $g=g^*$ 处的谱特性（如非对角化漂移矩阵）、动力学响应（自相关与协方差函数的时间演化），并结合无序平均、哈密顿映射（至Hatano-Nelson模型）以及Harada-Sasa关系计算稳态热流。

Result: 1. 在 $g=g^*$ 处出现高阶例外点，导致本征结构合并；2. 自相关与协方差函数在EP处表现出带有多项式修正的指数弛豫，其形式依赖于EP阶数和空间结构；3. 完全非对称下的多体链呈现‘伪平衡’态，稳态分布可分解，且系统等价于 $N/2$ 个独立复OU过程；4. 稳态热耗散有解析表达式，且存在边界制冷效应：边界粒子随非互易性增强从热源转变为冷源。

Conclusion: 非互易性不仅能破坏细致平衡，还可通过例外点显著改变系统的动力学和热力学行为，揭示了非厄米物理与随机热力学之间的深刻联系，并为调控活性系统中的能量输运提供了新机制。

Abstract: Non-reciprocal interactions are ubiquitous in active, biological, and disordered systems, generically driving them out of equilibrium. Here, we introduce a hierarchy of non-reciprocally interacting Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (NROU) models governed by a tunable non-reciprocity parameter $g$. At a special point $g=g^*$, the drift matrix becomes non-diagonalizable, realizing exceptional points (EP's) of different orders, where eigenvalues and eigenvectors simultaneously coalesce. The hierarchy encompasses non-reciprocally coupled dimers, their disordered counterparts, and a many-body chain exactly mapping onto the paradigmatic Hatano-Nelson model in the arena of non-Hermitian quantum systems. For the disordered model, we show that the distribution of the EP location $g^*$ across disorder realizations develops a universal edge singularity precisely at the clean-system EP, and is manifestly non-self-averaging. Across all models, we find that at the EP, the usual exponential relaxation of the autocorrelation and covariance functions is dressed by a polynomial-in-time prefactor whose degree is set by the order of the EP and whose detailed structure encodes the spatial architecture of the chain. At complete asymmetry, the many-body chain exhibits ``pseudo-equilibrium'': its steady-state distribution factorizes into equilibrium-like single-particle measures despite a nonzero steady-state current. Moreover, the $N$-particle interacting system decomposes into $N/2$ independent complex OU processes. Finally, using the Harada-Sasa relation, we obtain a closed-form expression for the total steady-state heat dissipation and uncover a boundary refrigeration effect, in which the boundary particles switch from acting as a hot to a cold reservoir as the non-reciprocity is tuned.

</details>
