### [146] [On tail-robust autocovariance matrix estimation for high-dimensional and potentially nonstationary time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04418)
*Haotian Xu,Stephane Guerrier,Runze Li,Yuan Ke*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究了在重尾、高维、非线性时间依赖和潜在非平稳性条件下时间序列的自协方差矩阵估计与推断问题，提出了两种鲁棒估计方法，并建立了非渐近理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际时间序列数据中，常存在重尾分布、高维特征以及时变性和复杂依赖结构，传统协方差估计方法表现不佳，因此需要发展适用于更广泛场景的鲁棒估计与推断方法。

Method: 提出两种元素级鲁棒估计方法：Huber M-估计和截断估计；基于函数依赖下的新型Bernstein型不等式建立非渐近误差界；进一步建立高维高斯逼近与乘子自助法用于统计推断。

Result: 所提估计量在矩阵最大范数下具有sharp误差界；建立了适用于潜在非平稳过程的高维高斯逼近和自助法理论；理论结果明确依赖样本量、维度、矩条件和时间依赖强度；数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的尾部鲁棒自协方差矩阵估计与推断方法在重尾、高维、非线性依赖和非平稳时间序列中具有良好的理论保证和实际表现，可用于真实宏观经济数据分析等应用场景。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the autocovariance matrix estimation and inference problems under heavy-tailedness, high-dimensionality, general nonlinear temporal dependence, and potentially nonstationarity of time series. We consider two types of tail-robust autocovariance matrix estimation methods: the element-wise Huber's $M$-estimator and a computationally more efficient element-wise truncated estimator. Both estimators are designed to achieve sharp error bounds in matrix max-norm. The nonasymptotic properties of these estimators are proved based on new variants of Bernstein-type inequalities under functional dependence for the potentially nonstationary processes which may be of independent interest. Moreover, we prove a high-dimensional Gaussian approximation result, as a limiting distribution, for our element-wise truncated autocovariance estimator. A Gaussian multiplier bootstrap result is also given to facilitate the practicality. Our theoretical results are nonasymptotic, which gives explicit error bounds in terms of the sample size, dimensionality, moments, and the strength of temporal dependence. Numerical evidence is provided to support our theoretical results. Finally, we illustrate the benefits of the proposed methodology for detecting change points in monthly macroeconomic data.

</details>


### [147] [Visualizing Class Specific Heterogeneous Tendencies using R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04608)
*Mariko Takagishi,Michel van de Velden*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文介绍了R包mccca，用于实现多类聚类对应分析（MCCCA），以识别和可视化不同“类”中的异质性趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了帮助研究者更好地识别和理解在不同类别中观测个体的异质性结构，开发一个易于使用的R工具。

Method: 基于MCCCA方法，利用外部变量定义类别，使用主动变量提取类特定的观测聚类，并通过低维空间进行可视化。

Result: 提供了mcca R包的具体应用示例，并通过两个数据集展示了如何执行MCCCA及其结果的可视化。

Conclusion: mccca包为进行多类聚类对应分析提供了一个有效的工具，有助于揭示复杂数据中与类别相关的结构特征。

Abstract: In this paper we introduce the R package mccca, which implements multiple-class cluster correspondence analysis (MCCCA) proposed in M.Takagishi et al., (2022). MCCCA is a statistical method that identifies and visualizes heterogeneous tendencies specific to ``classes'' (e.g., gender and nationality) in a low dimensional space. In MCCCA, two kinds of variables, external and active variables, are distinguished. External variables directly define classes whereas the active variables are used to derive class-specific clusters of observations (individuals). In this paper, we show how to apply MCCCA and how to visualize its results using mccca. We illustrate the procedure for performing MCCCA by applying it to two data sets.

</details>


### [148] [Time-dependent two-way partial AUC and partial Youden Index estimator for right censored data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04633)
*Arpan Sanyal,Sudheesh Kumar KattumannilSudheesh Kumar Kattumannil,Ayon Ganguly*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对右删失数据的非参数时间依赖性部分AUC估计方法，并讨论了相关的部分Youden指数及最优生物标志物截断点估计器，通过模拟研究和真实数据验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生存分析中，由于疾病状态随时间变化以及存在删失个体，传统ROC分析面临挑战，因此需要发展适用于右删失数据的时间依赖性ROC分析方法。

Method: 提出了非参数估计法来估计右删失数据下的时间依赖性双向部分AUC，并讨论了部分Youden指数及其对应的最优生物标志物截断点估计方法，通过大量模拟研究评估其小样本表现。

Result: 模拟研究表明所提出的非参数估计量能有效处理右删失问题，且在有限样本下表现良好；并通过两个真实数据集（原发性胆汁性肝硬化研究和乳腺癌国际 consortium 试验）验证了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法为评估生存数据中的生物标志物预测性能提供了有效的非参数工具，尤其适用于存在右删失的情况。

Abstract: In medical research, it is often of interest to evaluate the predictive performance of a biomarker. Statistical approaches based on the Receiver Operating Characteristic (ROC) curve and its summary measures, such as the area under the curve (AUC) and the Youden index, are widely used to evaluate the prognostic performance of these biomarkers. In time-to-event studies, ROC analysis poses additional challenges due to change in disease status over time and the presence of censored individuals. To address these issues, time-dependent ROC curves were introduced. In this paper, we propose a non-parametric estimator of the time-dependent two-way partial AUC for right-censored data. We also discuss the partial Youden index and the associated optimal biomarker cutoff estimator for the right-censored data. We conduct an extensive simulation study to investigate the finite sample performance of the proposed estimators. The simulation study indicates that the proposed non-parametric estimators efficiently account for right censoring. Finally, we illustrate the proposed methods using two real data sets, one from the Primary Biliary Cirrhosis study and the other from the Molecular Taxonomy of Breast Cancer International Consortium trial.

</details>


### [149] [A Quantile-Based Kumaraswamy-Teissier autoregressive moving average models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04736)
*Kamana Mishra,Tanmay Kayal,Sarita Azad*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分位数的Kumaraswamy-Teissier自回归滑动平均（KTARMA）模型，用于正向时间序列建模。该模型通过扩展的Kumaraswamy-Teissier分布和观测驱动框架，显式地将分布的随机部分参数化为其条件分位数，并利用ARMA型结构捕捉时间依赖性。研究实现了条件最大似然推断，开发了诊断与预测方法，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟验证了估计量的小样本性能。实际应用中，KTARMA模型在西北喜马拉雅地区月降雨量预测中表现出优越性，相较于KARMA和βARMA模型，在所有区域均取得更低的均方误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更灵活且准确地建模正向值时间序列（如降雨量），尤其是在存在时间变化协变量和复杂动态依赖的情况下，传统模型可能无法充分捕捉数据特征，因此需要一种新的、基于分位数的参数化方法以提升建模与预测能力。

Method: 提出KTARMA模型，采用扩展的Kumaraswamy-Teissier分布，在观测驱动框架下将分布的ρ-分位数作为建模对象；系统部分使用链接函数映射ARMA型结构到条件分位数；采用条件最大似然法进行推断，并导出得分向量与信息矩阵；发展模型诊断与预测工具；通过蒙特卡洛模拟评估小样本性能；应用于实际降雨数据，结合自组织映射划分区域，并引入大气变量与气候指数作为协变量。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟显示所提估计量在不同参数设置和分位数水平下具有良好的有限样本表现；在实际降雨数据中，KTARMA模型在四个同质区域的预测表现优于KARMA和βARMA模型，表现出更低的均方误差和更强的预测竞争力。

Conclusion: KTARMA模型为正向时间序列提供了一个灵活且高效的建模工具，尤其适用于具有非线性动态、外部协变量和关注特定分位数预测的应用场景，具备良好的理论性质与实际应用前景。

Abstract: This paper introduces a quantile-based Kumaraswamy-Teissier autoregressive moving average (KTARMA) model for positive-valued time series. Leveraging the flexibility of the extended Kumaraswamy-Teissier distribution within an observation-driven framework, the random component of the distribution is conditioned on the historical process and time-varying covariates, and is parameterized explicitly via its $ρ$-th conditional quantile, where $ρ\in (0,1)$. To capture temporal dependence, the systematic component maps an ARMA-type structure to this conditional quantile via an appropriate link function. For inference, we implement a conditional maximum likelihood framework and derive explicit analytical expressions for the resulting score vector and conditional information matrix, followed by the development of model diagnostic and forecasting procedures. The finite-sample performance of the developed estimators is evaluated through a Monte Carlo simulation study across various parameter configurations and quantile levels. Finally, the practical utility of the study is demonstrated by modeling monthly rainfall data over the Northwest Himalayas (2001-2025), where 525 grids are grouped into four homogeneous zones using a Self-Organizing Map and relevant atmospheric variables and large-scale climate indices are incorporated as predictive regressors. Out-of-sample forecasting evaluations reveal that the KTARMA model delivers highly competitive predictive performance, achieving consistently lower mean squared errors across all identified zones compared to KARMA and $β$ARMA models.

</details>


### [150] [A Strictly Proper Scoring-Rule Theory for Calibrating Stochastic Car-Following Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04988)
*Shirui Zhou,Shiteng Zheng,Junzhe Ding,Rui Jiang,Junfang Tian*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了随机校准的评分规则理论，指出传统基于轨迹误差的目标函数在随机交通仿真模型校准中的局限性，并推荐使用严格适当的评分规则（如能量得分）进行校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机跟车模型常使用源自确定性建模的轨迹误差目标进行校准，忽视了输出分布的变异性与时间依赖性，导致参数估计偏差和模型识别问题。

Method: 建立随机校准的评分规则理论，分析MRMean-I、MRMean-II和MRMin等常见目标函数的性质，并通过带有加速度噪声和随机期望车头时距的随机智能驾驶员模型进行实验验证；推荐使用精确最大似然法或基于模拟的无偏估计器来优化严格适当的评分规则（如能量得分）。

Result: MRMean-I会使随机离散趋于零，MRMean-II无法识别仅影响离散的参数，MRMin的总体目标随模拟数量变化；能量得分在所评估的基于模拟的目标中表现出最优的留出集分布预测性能，但两种模型仍存在带宽过窄和忽略持续扰动的问题。

Conclusion: 严格适当性是有效校准目标的关键属性，可将其与参数可识别性和模型充分性区分开来；该理论适用于随机交通仿真模型的向量输出，为随机模型校准提供了理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: Problem definition: Fixed parameters and inputs in a stochastic simulator induce a distribution over complete trajectories, not one trajectory. Calibration must assess this distribution, including variability and temporal dependence, against observations. Yet stochastic car-following models are commonly calibrated with trajectory-error objectives inherited from deterministic modelling. Methodology/results: We establish a scoring-rule theory of stochastic calibration. Strict propriety requires the data-generating distribution to uniquely minimise expected score. MRMean-I, the average run-wise error, drives separable stochastic spread to zero; MRMean-II, the error of the ensemble-mean trajectory, cannot identify a parameter that changes only spread; and MRMin, the error of the closest simulated run, has a population target that changes with ensemble size. These results are confirmed for stochastic Intelligent Driver Model extensions with additive acceleration noise and random desired headway. We recommend exact maximum likelihood when the correct transition density is available; otherwise, an unbiased simulation-based estimator of a strictly proper score. The energy score meets this requirement and gives the best held-out distributional prediction among the evaluated simulation-based objectives, although both models retain too-narrow bands and miss persistent disturbances. Implications:Strict propriety separates a valid calibration target from parameter identifiability and model adequacy. The theory applies to vector-valued outputs from stochastic transportation simulators; the car-following experiments illustrate its scope.

</details>


### [151] [Copula-Based Bivariate Kumaraswamy-Teissier Distributions: Modeling Temperature-Rainfall Dependence and Compound Extremes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04740)
*Kamana Mishra,Tanmay Kayal,Sarita Azad*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本研究提出了两种新的双变量分布模型，通过结合Kumaraswamy-Teissier边缘分布与Clayton和Gumbel Copula结构，用于联合建模温度和降雨量。引入旋转Copula变体以捕捉正负依赖关系，并利用最大似然法和IFM方法估计参数。模型在西北喜马拉雅地区的气象数据上表现优异，能有效刻画复合极端事件的依赖结构，并用于计算单变量、联合及条件重现期，提升山地气候风险评估能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为准确刻画温度与降雨之间的复杂依赖关系，尤其是在山地等气候多变区域，现有双变量模型存在局限，因此需要构建更具灵活性和适应性的联合分布模型。

Method: 采用Kumaraswamy-Teissier分布作为边缘分布，结合Clayton和Gumbel Copula及其旋转形式（90°、180°、270°），使用最大似然估计和IFM方法进行参数估计，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟评估小样本性能。

Result: 所提模型在西北喜马拉雅地区数据上优于现有模型，能够有效捕捉上下尾部及非对称依赖结构，并成功应用于夏季和冬季的复合极端事件风险分析。

Conclusion: 该框架能更真实地表示水文气候变量间的依赖关系，为山地地区极端气温与降水事件的风险评估提供了稳健工具。

Abstract: This study proposes two novel bivariate distributions for jointly modeling temperature and rainfall by integrating Kumaraswamy-Teissier marginals with Clayton and Gumbel copula structures. To capture a wide range of dependence patterns, including both positive and negative associations, rotated copula variants (90°, 180°, and 270°) are incorporated along with their corresponding tail dependence characteristics. Model parameters are estimated using maximum likelihood and the inference functions for margins (IFM) approach, and their finite-sample performance is assessed through a comprehensive Monte Carlo simulation study. The proposed models are applied to monthly gridded temperature and rainfall data from the Northwest Himalayas, a region characterized by complex hydro-climatic variability. Comparative analysis demonstrates that the proposed framework outperforms several existing bivariate models and effectively captures lower-tail, upper-tail, and asymmetric dependence structures across summer and winter seasons. Based on the selected best-fitting copula models, univariate, joint, and conditional return periods are derived to quantify the risk of compound extremes. The results highlight the capability of the proposed approach to provide a more realistic representation of hydro-climatic dependence and offer a robust framework for assessing the risk of extreme temperature and rainfall events in mountainous regions.

</details>


### [152] [Variational Inference for Functional Data Clustering via Dirichlet Process Mixtures with Correlated Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04853)
*Chengqian Xian*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于贝叶斯模型的功能性数据聚类方法，能够处理未知聚类数量和时间相关观测，通过B样条基展开和Ornstein-Uhlenbeck协方差结构建模，并使用变分EM算法实现高效推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有功能性聚类方法在处理时间依赖性和未知聚类数量方面存在不足，需要一种能同时处理这些挑战的灵活且高效的模型。

Method: 采用B样条基展开表示聚类特异性均值函数，用Ornstein-Uhlenbeck协方差结构建模曲线内依赖性，结合截断狄利克雷过程混合模型推断聚类数，并开发变分EM算法进行后验近似。

Result: 模拟研究表明该方法在均值函数设定正确与误设下均表现良好，聚类和参数估计结果与MCMC一致但计算成本显著更低，在加拿大日气温曲线应用中展现出识别可解释功能聚类的能力。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效处理具有时间依赖的功能性数据聚类问题，自动推断聚类数量，兼具准确性与计算效率，适用于实际应用。

Abstract: We propose a Bayesian model-based approach for clustering functional data with an unknown number of clusters and temporally correlated observations. Cluster-specific mean functions are represented using B-spline basis expansions, while within-curve dependence is modeled through an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck covariance structure. A truncated Dirichlet process mixture is used to infer the effective number of clusters, and a variational EM algorithm is developed for efficient posterior approximation. Simulation studies show that the proposed method performs well under both correctly specified and misspecified mean-function settings and compares favorably with several existing functional clustering methods. Comparisons with MCMC indicate that the variational approximation yields consistent clustering and parameter estimates but at substantially lower computational cost. An application to Canadian daily temperature curves further demonstrates the practical usefulness of the method in identifying interpretable functional clusters while accounting for temporal dependence.

</details>


### [153] [Goodness-of-fit testing for the Pareto type-I distribution based on a mean residual life characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04933)
*Shivshankar Nila,Ishapathik Das,N. Balakrishna*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于平均剩余寿命特征的U统计量方法，用于检验帕累托分布的拟合优度，并验证了其在多种数据下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于重尾数据在实际应用中频繁出现极端观测值，对帕累托分布的拟合优度进行有效检验具有重要意义。

Method: 利用U统计量理论构建基于平均剩余寿命特征的检验统计量，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟评估其在有限样本下的表现。

Result: 所提出的检验方法能够良好地控制名义显著性水平，并在多种替代假设下表现出较强的检验功效。

Conclusion: 该方法在丹麦火灾保险损失和污染数据集中得到了有效验证，适用于实际重尾数据分析。

Abstract: The statistical analysis of heavy-tailed data has received considerable attention because extreme observations frequently arise in many practical applications. The Pareto type-I distribution is a fundamental heavy-tailed model used in economics, finance, actuarial science, insurance, reliability, and extreme value analysis. In this paper, we propose novel goodness-of-fit tests for the Pareto distribution using a mean residual life characterization. The test statistic is constructed using U-statistic theory, and its asymptotic behaviour is established under both the null and alternative hypotheses. Its finite-sample performance is evaluated through Monte Carlo simulations using maximum-likelihood and method-of-moments estimation and compared with existing tests. The results show that the proposed test controls the nominal significance level and performs competitively in terms of power across a broad range of alternatives. Finally, the proposed methodology is illustrated using the Danish fire insurance loss and pollution datasets.

</details>


### [154] [Treatment persistence drives estimator performance in longitudinal causal inference based on observational data: A simulation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04940)
*Sergio Gaiotti,Sara Poletto,Enrico Longato,Erica Tavazzi,Martina Vettoretti*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本研究通过结构因果模型模拟数据，探讨了纵向治疗动态对因果效应估计的影响，发现治疗持续性是影响估计器表现的主要因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着纵向临床数据的增多，研究者面临时间依赖性混杂和动态治疗决策的挑战，需要理解不同因果估计方法在不同治疗动态下的表现差异。

Method: 使用结构因果模型（SCM）模拟包含时间依赖性混杂、二元治疗和吸收性二元结局的数据，在9种不同情景下比较三种基线和两种纵向估计器与蒙特卡洛真实风险比（RR）的差异。

Result: 治疗持续性显著影响估计器行为：高持续性降低基线与持续干预估计值之间的差异（基线IPTW/TMLE的平均相对偏差从低持续性的61%降至10%），而低持续性导致实际 positivity 问题并增加纵向估计器的变异性（纵向IPTW的95%区间宽度从0.22增至0.46，LTMLE从0.20增至0.36）。

Conclusion: 估计器的表现应结合目标干预措施和实际治疗过程共同解读，治疗持续性是决定方法选择的关键因素。

Abstract: Longitudinal clinical data are increasingly available, offering opportunities to study treatment effects over time but also raising challenges related to time-varying confounding and evolving treatment decisions. We investigate how longitudinal treatment dynamics affect causal effect estimation when baseline and longitudinal methods target different causal estimands. Using a structural causal model (SCM), we simulate data with time-varying confounding, binary treatment, and an absorbing binary outcome under 9 scenarios combining functional complexity and treatment persistence. We compare three baseline and two longitudinal estimators against Monte Carlo ground-truth risk ratios (RRs) under sustained treatment regimes. Results show that treatment persistence is the main driver of estimator behaviour. High persistence reduces the discrepancy between baseline and sustained-regime estimands, making baseline estimators closer to the sustained-regime ground truth (average relative deviation of baseline IPTW/TMLE decreasing from 61% under low persistence to 10% under high persistence), whereas low persistence induces practical positivity challenges and increases the variability of longitudinal estimators, with empirical 95% interval widths increasing from 0.22 to 0.46 for longitudinal IPTW and from 0.20 to 0.36 for LTMLE when moving from high to low persistence. These findings emphasise that estimator performance should be interpreted jointly with the target intervention and the treatment process generating the observed data.

</details>


### [155] [Finite Mixtures of Generalized Estimating Equations for Clustering Multivariate Correlated Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04960)
*Shonosuke Sugasawa,Francis K. C. Hui*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MixGEE的有限混合广义估计方程方法，用于对具有相关多元结果的观测单元进行聚类分析，特别适用于生态学中的生物区划问题，能够同时考虑物种间的相关性并无需指定完整的联合分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生态学、社会科学等领域中，常需对具有多变量相关结果的观测单位进行聚类，以识别具有相同“结果特征”的组别；本文动机源于生态学中的生物区划，旨在根据物种分布特征将样地划分为生物区，并探讨环境协变量对分区的影响。

Method: 提出有限混合广义估计方程（MixGEE），通过区域特定的工作相关结构建模物种间相关性；构造基于估计方程大样本分布的伪后验概率，采用迭代算法交替更新隶属概率和求解加权估计方程；使用交叉验证选择区域数量，并采用聚类Dirichlet随机权重自助法进行不确定性量化。

Result: 模拟研究表明MixGEE在不同相关结构下具有可靠的估计与推断性能，且在组数选择上比忽略依赖的方法更稳定；应用于凯尔盖朗高原鱼类存在-缺失数据时，识别出三个具有异质性发生模式和站点内相关结构的鱼类群落特征。

Conclusion: MixGEE能够在不假设完整联合分布的情况下有效聚类多变量相关结果，适用于生物区划等实际问题，且能准确刻画区域特有的物种均值与相关结构，提升聚类稳定性与可解释性。

Abstract: Multivariate correlated outcomes occur across disciplines, including ecology, social sciences, and psychometrics. This paper focuses on clustering these outcomes across observational units, specifically, finding groups of units with the same ``outcome profile". Our motivation comes from bioregionalization in ecology, which aims to cluster sites into bioregions with the same species profiles, where site membership can depend on environmental or habitat covariates. To accomplish this, we propose finite mixtures of generalized estimating equations (MixGEE). Unlike existing approaches to model-based bioregionalization, MixGEE partitions sites into regions while accounting for between-species correlations through a region-specific working correlation structure. Thus, each region is characterized by a marginal species mean vector and a between-species correlation matrix. Unlike likelihood-based finite mixture models, MixGEE does not require a full joint distribution of the multivariate outcomes. Instead, we construct a pseudo-posterior probability for region membership motivated by the large-sample distribution of the estimating equation. This leads to an iterative algorithm alternating between updating these probabilities and solving weighted estimating equations. We determine the number of regions using cross-validation based on predictive performance for held-out sites and species components, and use a clustered Dirichlet random-weight bootstrap for uncertainty quantification. Simulations demonstrate reliable estimation and inference under various correlation structures and more stable selection of the number of groups than methods that ignore dependence. Applying MixGEE to presence--absence records of fish species around the Kerguelen Plateau reveals three distinct fish assemblage profiles with heterogeneous occurrence and within-site correlation patterns.

</details>


### [156] [On E-Backtesting: Generalizations and Sample Size Determination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05089)
*Dennis Oestmann,Thorsten Dickhaus*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种方法来确定在使用e-backtesting程序检测预期亏损低估时所需的样本量，利用e-statistic的结构推导出近似下界，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟比较实际应用中的样本需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了准确检测预期亏损（expected shortfall）的低估情况，需要确定合适的样本量以保证检验功效，特别是在VaR被正确估计但ES被低估的情况下。

Method: 通过分析e-backtesting中e-statistic的结构，将其近似为独立同分布的伯努利随机变量序列，从而推导所需样本量的近似下界，并用蒙特卡洛模拟验证结果。

Result: 得到了检测预期亏损低估所需样本量的近似下界，模拟结果显示该近似在一定条件下有效，并讨论了其局限性。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效估算e-backtesting中检测预期亏损低估所需的样本量，且可推广至构成Bayes对的风险度量。

Abstract: We present an approach for determining sample sizes required to detect underestimations of the expected shortfall with a prescribed power when applying the recently proposed e-backtesting procedure. We consider scenarios in which the value-at-risk at level $p$ is always estimated correctly, while the difference between the true expected shortfall and the value-at-risk is underestimated by a given factor $r$. We show that exploiting the structure of the backtest e-statistic proposed for backtesting the expected shortfall at level $p$ enables the derivation of approximate lower bounds for the required sample sizes by considering a sequence of independent and identically distributed Bernoulli random variables. We also discuss potential limitations of this approximation and compare the resulting sample size requirements with those obtained in practical applications using Monte Carlo simulations. Furthermore, we present generalizations of the e-backtesting procedure, in particular to risk measures which constitute Bayes pairs.

</details>


### [157] [Post-Corrected Raw-Score Martingale Posterior Sampling for von Mises-Fisher Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05096)
*Yi Xu,Xinye Chen,Sheng Jiang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种有限视界校准方法，用于原始分数鞅后验的校准，以von Mises-Fisher模型为例，并通过终端线性修正和高斯近似提高效率和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决原始分数鞅后验在校准过程中存在的协方差不匹配问题，尤其是在预测模拟与协方差校准之间的耦合导致的偏差。

Method: 基于最大似然估计生成预测路径，通过未预处理的得分增量更新自然参数；分离预测模拟与协方差校准，采用基于局部信息估计的终端线性修正，并引入高斯近似实现更高效的混合版本。

Result: 证明了固定样本量下的收敛性、高斯尾部近似的有限视界误差界，以及在局部正则性和一致终端校准下的Bernstein-von Mises极限；模拟显示尾部校正减少了截断引起的欠分散，实例验证了方向性不确定性的局部汇总效果。

Conclusion: 所提出的校准方法有效解决了协方差结构失配问题，结合终端修正与高斯近似，在理论保证与计算效率之间取得了良好平衡，适用于实际方向数据建模。

Abstract: We develop a finite-horizon calibration method for raw-score martingale posteriors, with von Mises--Fisher models as the main worked example. Starting from the maximum likelihood estimator, predictive paths are generated by simulating future observations from the current fitted model and updating the natural parameter by unpreconditioned score increments. The main methodological step is to separate predictive simulation from covariance calibration. Raw-score increments have Fisher-information covariance, whereas Bernstein--von Mises calibration requires inverse-information covariance. We therefore apply a terminal linear correction based on a local information estimate. For more efficient implementation, we also introduce a hybrid version that replaces the omitted tail of the infinite predictive continuation by a Gaussian approximation with matching leading-order quadratic variation. We prove fixed-n convergence, a finite-horizon approximation bound for the Gaussian tail, and a Bernstein--von Mises limit for the hybrid post-corrected sampler under local regularity and consistent terminal calibration. Simulations show that tail correction reduces truncation-induced underdispersion, and an OSCAR ocean-current example illustrates local directional uncertainty summaries.

</details>

### [158] [Quantum Circuit Learning for Volatility Modeling: Multifractal Analysis of Realized Volatility Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04569)
*Tetsuya Takaishi*

Main category: q-fin.CP

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于量子电路学习的比特币已实现波动率建模框架，并通过多重分形分析验证了其预测时间序列的统计特性，结果显示单量子比特电路能捕捉比特币波动的部分复杂特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了克服传统GARCH类模型需要预设波动函数形式和显式模型选择的问题，探索利用参数化量子电路直接从数据中学习波动率动态的可能性。

Method: 使用五分钟比特币价格数据构建日度已实现波动率，训练单量子比特参数化量子电路生成合成时间序列，并采用多重分形去趋势波动分析（MF-DFA）计算广义Hurst指数、奇异性谱和多重分形标度指数。

Result: 预测收益率序列显示h(2)≈0.5，具有近似随机动力学特征；预测与实证序列均表现出在随机打乱后仍部分持续的多重分形性；RV增量序列表现出显著反持久性（h(2)≈0.05–0.1），符合粗糙波动率假说。

Conclusion: 简单的单量子比特参数化量子电路能够定性地捕捉比特币波动率动态中的某些观测特性，表明量子电路学习在金融时间序列建模中具有潜力。

Abstract: Herein, we propose a quantum circuit learning framework for modeling the realized volatility (RV) of Bitcoin and investigate the statistical properties of the predicted time series through multifractal analysis. Unlike conventional GARCH-type models, which require a pre-specified functional form for the volatility process, a parameterized quantum circuit directly approximates the volatility function from empirical data, eliminating the need for explicit model selection. Using five-minute Bitcoin price data, we construct daily RV, train a single-qubit parameterized quantum circuit, and generate a long synthetic time series from the optimized quantum circuit. Multifractal Detrended Fluctuation Analysis is applied to calculate the generalized Hurst exponent $h(q)$, the singularity spectrum $f(α)$, and the multifractal scaling exponent $τ(q)$. The predicted return series exhibits $h(2)\approx 0.5$, consistent with near-random dynamics, and both the predicted and the empirical return series display multifractality that partially persists after random shuffling. The increment series of RV shows pronounced anti-persistence with $h(2)\approx 0.05$--$0.1$, consistent with the rough volatility hypothesis. These results demonstrate that a simple single-qubit parameterized quantum circuit captures qualitatively some observed properties in Bitcoin volatility dynamics.

</details>
